\# 1. Purpose

H1 menetapkan constitutional foundation bagi evolusi

Governance & Compliance pada PD-03.

H1 memastikan bahwa governance dapat berkembang mengikuti:

\- perubahan kebutuhan AIOS;

\- governance evidence;

\- performance findings;

\- compliance requirements;

\- architecture evolution;

\- lifecycle evolution;

\- cross-platform dependencies;

\- organizational change;

\- future expansion;

tanpa menghilangkan:

\- Governance Authority;

\- organizational ownership;

\- architecture authority;

\- operational ownership;

\- Platform autonomy;

\- traceability;

\- accountability;

\- compliance integrity.

H1 menjadi constitutional parent bagi seluruh

\*\*Platform Evolution Architecture\*\* pada Part H.

H1 tidak menggantikan detail yang menjadi tanggung jawab

H2–H10.

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

PD-03 memiliki primary responsibility terhadap:

\- Policy;

\- Standards;

\- Control;

\- Certification;

dalam domain Governance & Compliance.

Primary responsibility tersebut tidak memberikan PD-03

ownership atas technical design atau operational execution

milik Platform lain.

Canonical boundary:

\`\`\`text

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

│

├── Policy

├── Standards

├── Control

└── Certification

│

▼

Governance Evolution

Governance evolution harus tetap berada dalam Governance Authority domain PD-03.

PD-03 tidak memperoleh ownership baru hanya karena governance model mengalami evolution.

Baseline secara eksplisit membedakan Governance responsibility dari execution ownership.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

3\. Evolution Constitutional Principle

Governance evolution merupakan mekanisme untuk memastikan bahwa Governance & Compliance tetap relevan, efektif, terkendali, dan dapat dipertanggungjawabkan ketika AIOS berkembang.

Evolution tidak berarti perubahan tanpa batas.

Evolution harus:

1\. memiliki legitimate reason;

2\. memiliki identifiable driver;

3\. dapat dianalisis dampaknya;

4\. memiliki owner;

5\. memiliki authority yang sesuai;

6\. melalui review yang applicable;

7\. menghasilkan traceable decision;

8\. mempertahankan governance integrity;

9\. mempertahankan Platform autonomy;

10\. menghasilkan evidence.

⸻

4\. Evolution Principles

4.1 Stability Before Expansion

Platform atau governance capability yang belum stabil tidak boleh diperluas secara tidak terkendali.

Evolution harus terlebih dahulu memastikan bahwa kondisi existing cukup stabil untuk menerima perubahan.

Prinsip:

Stability

↓

Assessment

↓

Controlled Evolution

↓

Expansion

Expansion tidak boleh menjadi substitute untuk memperbaiki kelemahan fundamental.

Source Status: Source-supported.

Baseline secara eksplisit menetapkan:

Stability Before Expansion

sebagai salah satu evolution principles.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

4.2 Reference Before Duplication

Evolution harus menggunakan canonical reference yang sudah tersedia sebelum membuat definisi atau mechanism baru.

Jika governance requirement sudah memiliki canonical source, evolution tidak boleh membuat competing definition tanpa alasan yang sah.

Prinsip:

Existing Canonical Source

↓

Assess Reuse

↓

Adapt if Necessary

↓

Create New Only When Justified

Source Status: Source-supported.

⸻

4.3 Controlled Change

Perubahan mayor terhadap governance harus dikendalikan.

Minimal requirement:

• reason for change;

• impact analysis;

• owner;

• approval;

• versioning.

Model:

Change Reason

↓

Impact Analysis

↓

Owner

↓

Approval

↓

Versioned Change

Source Status: Source-supported.

Baseline secara eksplisit menetapkan requirement tersebut untuk major change.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

4.4 Backward Compatibility

Governance evolution harus sedapat mungkin mempertahankan compatibility terhadap Platform dan governance relationship yang sudah ada.

Jika compatibility tidak dapat dipertahankan, evolution harus menyediakan mekanisme migration yang dapat ditelusuri.

Existing Governance State

│

├── Compatible

│ ↓

│ Controlled Adoption

│

└── Incompatible

↓

Migration

Source Status: Source-supported.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

4.5 Canonical First

Jika terdapat konflik antar artefak governance, evolution harus menggunakan applicable authority precedence.

Reference precedence yang tersedia dalam source:

Executive Decision

↓

Volume 0

↓

Volume Platform

↓

Supporting Documents

Evolution tidak boleh menggunakan supporting document untuk secara informal mengalahkan canonical authority.

Source Status: Source-supported.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

• developer execution;

Governance

Dependency

11\. Governance Evolution Ownership

Governance evolution harus memiliki ownership yang eksplisit.

Setiap material evolution matter wajib dapat menjawab:

• siapa mengusulkan;

• siapa memiliki concern;

• siapa melakukan assessment;

• siapa melakukan review;

• siapa memiliki authority;

• siapa memberikan approval;

• siapa menjalankan implementation;

• siapa memverifikasi outcome.

Canonical ownership principle:

Governance Owner

│

├── Governance Decision

├── Governance Control

└── Governance Assurance

Applicable Domain Owner

│

└── Domain Execution

Evolution tidak boleh menciptakan ambiguous ownership.

⸻

12\. Evidence-Based Evolution

Governance evolution harus berbasis evidence yang relevan.

Potential evidence sources:

• governance findings;

• compliance findings;

• review outcomes;

• performance evidence;

• lifecycle findings;

• integration findings;

• architecture decisions;

• audit evidence;

• recurring operational friction;

• documented change requirements.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri kepada source dan context yang relevan.

Model:

Evidence

↓

Finding / Signal

↓

Evolution Need

↓

Assessment

↓

Decision

H1 tidak menentukan detailed evidence schema.

Detail tersebut diturunkan kepada H2–H7 sesuai scope masing-masing.

Governance evolution harus menggunakan authority yang telah ditetapkan oleh Governance Architecture.

H1 tidak menciptakan authority baru.

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Jika suatu evolution membutuhkan authority yang berada di luar PD-03, matter tersebut harus menggunakan applicable cross-platform atau higher-level governance mechanism.

Tidak diperbolehkan:

Evolution Need

↓

Self-Authorized Change

14\. Evolution and Platform Autonomy

PD-03 harus mempertahankan autonomy Platform lain.

Governance evolution dapat mengubah:

• governance requirement;

• policy;

• standards;

• certification requirement;

• compliance mechanism;

• review mechanism.

Namun perubahan tersebut tidak secara otomatis memindahkan execution ownership.

Model:

PD-03

Governance Requirement

↓

Applicable Platform

↓

Domain Owner

↓

Execution / Remediation

Canonical principle:

Governance responsibility does not automatically transfer domain ownership.

15\. Evolution Traceability

Setiap material governance evolution harus dapat ditelusuri dari:

Driver

↓

Context

↓

Evidence

↓

Evolution Need

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Decision

↓

Approval

↓

Implementation

↓

Validation

↓

Outcome

Traceability harus memungkinkan reviewer memahami:

• mengapa evolution diperlukan;

• authority apa yang digunakan;

• siapa owner-nya;

• apa yang berubah;

• apa dampaknya;

• bagaimana perubahan divalidasi.

Governance traceability merupakan bagian dari constitutional governance PD-03.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

16\. Evolution Integrity

Governance evolution dianggap constitutionally valid hanya apabila:

1\. berada dalam authorized domain;

2\. memiliki legitimate driver;

3\. memiliki evidence atau rationale yang dapat ditelusuri;

4\. memiliki applicable owner;

5\. menggunakan authority yang sah;

6\. mempertahankan accountability;

7\. mempertahankan Platform autonomy;

8\. mempertimbangkan impact;

9\. memiliki change control;

10\. menghasilkan documented outcome.

Tidak diperbolehkan melakukan evolution hanya berdasarkan:

• preference;

• convenience;

• undocumented assumption;

• organizational politics;

• undocumented technical requirement.

⸻

17\. Evolution Safeguards

Setiap material evolution harus mempertimbangkan minimal:

17.1 Governance Integrity

Apakah perubahan menjaga integrity governance?

17.2 Authority Integrity

Apakah authority tetap jelas dan sah?

17.3 Ownership Integrity

Apakah domain ownership tetap jelas?

17.4 Platform Autonomy

Apakah perubahan mengambil alih domain Platform lain?

17.5 Traceability

Apakah alasan dan consequence dapat ditelusuri?

17.6 Compatibility

Apakah existing governance relationship tetap kompatibel?

17.7 Migration

Jika compatibility tidak mungkin, apakah migration path tersedia?

17.8 Stability

Apakah perubahan dilakukan sebelum governance capability mencapai kondisi yang cukup stabil?

⸻

18\. Evolution Constitutional Contract

H1 menetapkan kontrak berikut:

PD-03 MAY:

\- govern authorized evolution;

\- define governance requirements;

\- evolve governance policy;

\- evolve governance standards;

\- evolve governance controls;

\- evolve certification mechanisms;

\- evolve compliance mechanisms;

\- initiate governed improvement;

\- coordinate cross-platform governance evolution.

PD-03 MUST:

\- preserve authority boundaries;

\- preserve domain ownership;

\- preserve Platform autonomy;

\- use evidence;

\- maintain traceability;

\- control material change;

\- use applicable approval;

\- document evolution outcome.

PD-03 MUST NOT:

\- self-authorize material change;

\- take technical ownership;

\- take operational ownership;

\- replace Architecture Authority;

\- replace domain owners;

\- bypass applicable review;

\- bypass canonical authority;

\- create uncontrolled governance duplication.

⸻

22\. Evolution and Future Expansion

Future expansion harus dilakukan setelah:

• current capability sufficiently stable;

• dependencies understood;

• governance impact assessed;

• authority established;

• compatibility considered;

• ownership defined.

Model:

Stable Existing Capability

↓

Expansion Assessment

↓

Impact Analysis

↓

Authorization

↓

Controlled Expansion

Expansion tidak boleh menghilangkan existing governance integrity.

⸻

23\. Constitutional Non-Regression

Governance evolution tidak boleh secara sengaja menurunkan constitutional integrity tanpa explicit authorized decision.

Evolution harus menjaga:

• authority clarity;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• traceability;

• compliance;

• Platform autonomy;

• canonical consistency.

Jika regression tidak dapat dihindari, harus tersedia:

• explicit rationale;

• impact assessment;

• authority decision;

• migration or mitigation;

• documented consequence.

⸻

26\. Success Conditions

H1 dianggap constitutionally successful apabila:

• evolution memiliki legitimate constitutional basis;

• evolution berada dalam Governance Authority domain;

• authority boundary tetap jelas;

• domain ownership tetap jelas;

• Platform autonomy tetap terjaga;

• material changes memiliki control;

• evidence dapat ditelusuri;

• major changes memiliki impact analysis;

• approval dapat diverifikasi;

• compatibility dipertimbangkan;

• migration tersedia bila compatibility tidak dapat dipertahankan;

• continuous improvement dapat dilakukan secara controlled;

• future expansion tidak dilakukan secara premature;

• child sections dapat diturunkan tanpa structural contradiction.

5\. Current Governance State

Evolution selalu dimulai dari kondisi yang sudah ada.

Current Governance State dapat mencakup:

• current governance constitution;

• current policies;

• current standards;

• current controls;

• current certification mechanisms;

• current compliance mechanisms;

• current authority;

• current accountability;

• current operating model;

• current governance relationships;

• current platform interfaces;

• current evidence;

• current known limitations.

Current State harus dipahami sebelum perubahan dirancang.

Prinsip:

Do Not Evolve What Has Not Been Understood.

Assessment harus memiliki reference terhadap state yang sedang berlaku.

⸻

6\. Desired Governance State

Evolution harus memiliki tujuan yang dapat dijelaskan.

Desired State dapat berupa:

• improved governance effectiveness;

• improved compliance;

• improved accountability;

• improved traceability;

• reduced governance friction;

• improved decision quality;

• improved review effectiveness;

• improved cross-platform coordination;

• improved certification;

• improved control;

• improved adaptability;

• controlled future expansion.

Desired State tidak boleh ditentukan hanya sebagai "lebih baik".

Evolution objective harus memiliki rationale yang dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

7\. State Delta

Evolution dapat dipahami sebagai perubahan terkontrol antara Current State dan Desired State.

Current State

│

│

▼

State Delta

│

▼

Desired State

State Delta harus menjawab:

0\. apa yang berubah;

0\. mengapa berubah;

0\. siapa yang terdampak;

0\. apa yang tidak berubah;

0\. apa risiko perubahan;

0\. apa dependency perubahan;

0\. bagaimana perubahan akan divalidasi.

H2 tidak menentukan detailed impact analysis method.

Detail tersebut diturunkan kepada H6 dan H7.

⸻

8\. Evolution Signal

Tidak semua perubahan dimulai dari proposal formal.

Evolution dapat dipicu oleh signal.

Potential signals:

• governance finding;

• compliance finding;

• performance degradation;

• repeated operational friction;

• architecture review finding;

• lifecycle finding;

• integration finding;

• audit finding;

• regulatory requirement;

• policy change;

• organizational change;

• dependency change;

• risk;

• future expansion requirement.

Signal hanya merupakan input.

Signal belum merupakan authorization.

Signal

≠

Decision

⸻

9\. Evolution Driver

Evolution Driver merupakan alasan substantif mengapa Governance & Compliance perlu dipertimbangkan untuk berubah.

Canonical distinction:

Evidence

↓

Signal

↓

Driver

↓

Evolution Need

Contoh:

Repeated Compliance Failure

↓

Compliance Signal

↓

Control Weakness

↓

Evolution Driver

↓

Governance Change Candidate

H3 memiliki responsibility untuk mendefinisikan dan mengklasifikasikan Evolution Drivers.

H2 hanya menetapkan posisi Driver dalam model.

⸻

10\. Evolution Need

Evolution Driver tidak otomatis menghasilkan perubahan.

Setelah driver diidentifikasi, harus ditentukan:

Is Evolution Actually Required?

Canonical decision concept:

Evolution Driver

↓

Assessment

│

├── No Evolution Required

│

├── Operational Improvement

│

├── Lifecycle Change

│

└── Governance Evolution

Ini penting untuk mencegah semua finding berubah menjadi structural governance change.

⸻

11\. Materiality Boundary

Tidak semua improvement memiliki materiality yang sama.

Evolution model membedakan minimal:

Minor Improvement

│

▼

Material Improvement

│

▼

Structural Evolution

│

▼

Expansion

Materiality menentukan seberapa besar governance mechanism yang diperlukan.

H2 tidak menetapkan threshold numerik.

Threshold dan classification mechanism menjadi concern H4 dan H5.

⸻

12\. Evolution Path Selection

Setelah Evolution Need dinilai, perubahan harus diarahkan ke path yang sesuai.

Canonical path:

Evolution Need

│

┌─────────────┼─────────────┐

│ │ │

▼ ▼ ▼

No Change Improvement Evolution

│

▼

Expansion

Path selection tidak boleh dilakukan berdasarkan preferensi informal.

Path harus didukung oleh:

• evidence;

• materiality;

• impact;

• authority;

• ownership;

• governance requirement.

⸻

13\. Improvement Path

Jika kebutuhan tidak membutuhkan structural evolution, maka improvement dapat dilakukan tanpa mengubah governance architecture secara material.

Finding

↓

Improvement

↓

Validation

↓

Improved Current State

Improvement tetap harus berada dalam existing authority.

Improvement tidak boleh digunakan untuk secara informal mengubah constitutional boundary.

⸻

14\. Evolution Path

Jika perubahan bersifat material, evolution path digunakan.

Evolution Need

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Decision

↓

Authorization

↓

Controlled Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Validation

Evolution path digunakan untuk perubahan yang memiliki material impact terhadap Governance & Compliance.

⸻

15\. Expansion Path

Expansion merupakan bentuk evolution yang memperluas governance capability, applicability, scope, atau integration.

Expansion hanya dapat dilakukan apabila:

• existing capability sufficiently stable;

• dependency understood;

• governance impact assessed;

• ownership defined;

• authority established;

• compatibility considered.

Canonical principle:

Stability

↓

Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Expansion

Expansion tidak boleh digunakan untuk menghindari stabilization.

⸻

16\. Controlled Evolution Loop

Evolution harus mengikuti closed-loop model:

1\. Observe

↓

2\. Identify

↓

3\. Assess

↓

4\. Decide

↓

5\. Authorize

↓

6\. Change

↓

7\. Validate

↓

8\. Learn

↓

9\. Observe Again

Loop tersebut membentuk continuous evolution mechanism.

⸻

17\. Observe

Observe berarti mengumpulkan evidence mengenai kondisi Governance & Compliance.

Potential sources:

• governance operation;

• governance review;

• compliance review;

• performance measurement;

• architecture review;

• lifecycle review;

• integration review;

• audit;

• incident;

• decision record;

• stakeholder feedback.

Observe tidak berarti langsung mengubah governance.

⸻

18\. Identify

Identify mengubah evidence menjadi signal atau finding yang dapat dianalisis.

Contoh:

Evidence

↓

Finding

↓

Pattern

↓

Potential Evolution Driver

Pattern recognition penting karena satu anomaly tidak selalu berarti structural governance weakness.

⸻

19\. Assess

Assessment menentukan:

• materiality;

• impact;

• urgency;

• dependency;

• affected owner;

• governance implication;

• compatibility;

• risk;

• expected benefit.

Assessment menghasilkan:

Evolution Assessment

Assessment tidak sama dengan decision.

Assessment

≠

Authorization

⸻

20\. Decide

Decision menentukan apakah evolution diperlukan dan bagaimana path yang applicable.

Possible outcomes:

No Change

Improvement

Controlled Evolution

Expansion

Escalation

Decision harus menggunakan authority yang berlaku.

H5 bertanggung jawab terhadap detailed Evolution Decision Governance.

⸻

21\. Authorize

Authorization memastikan perubahan memiliki authority yang sah.

H2 hanya menempatkan authorization dalam model.

H2 tidak menetapkan siapa final authority secara baru.

Authority berasal dari:

• Governance Constitution;

• Authority Governance;

• applicable decision governance;

• delegated authority;

• higher-level authority apabila diperlukan.

⸻

22\. Change

Change merupakan actual modification terhadap governance state.

Potential change objects:

• policy;

• standard;

• control;

• certification mechanism;

• compliance mechanism;

• governance relationship;

• governance process;

• governance capability;

• governance artifact.

Change harus controlled.

Minimum major-change discipline:

Reason

-

Impact Analysis

-

Owner

-

Approval

-

Version

⸻

23\. Implementation

Implementation menerjemahkan authorized change menjadi actual changed state.

Authorized Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Changed Governance State

Implementation tidak boleh mengubah scope authorization secara unilateral.

H8 bertanggung jawab terhadap detailed Evolution Implementation.

⸻

24\. Validation

Setelah implementation, perubahan harus divalidasi.

Validation menjawab:

• apakah intended change terjadi;

• apakah governance requirement terpenuhi;

• apakah compatibility tetap terjaga;

• apakah ownership tetap jelas;

• apakah unintended impact muncul;

• apakah expected outcome tercapai.

Implementation

↓

Validation

│

├── PASS

│

└── FAIL

Failure menghasilkan:

Rework

Rollback

Mitigation

Further Evolution

Escalation

sesuai authority dan control yang berlaku.

⸻

Old State

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Validation

↓

New State

46\. Evolution Model Invariants

Invariant berikut harus selalu dipertahankan:

Invariant 1

Evolution must have a reason.

Invariant 2

Evolution must remain within authority.

Invariant 3

Evolution must preserve ownership clarity.

Invariant 4

Evolution must preserve Platform autonomy.

Invariant 5

Material change must be controlled.

Invariant 6

Evolution must be traceable.

Invariant 7

Evolution must be validated.

Invariant 8

Expansion follows sufficient stability.

Invariant 9

Canonical authority remains authoritative.

Invariant 10

Evolution does not automatically create new authority.

50\. Evolution Model Canonical Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance evolves through a controlled, evidence-based, authority-bounded feedback model that observes the current governance state, identifies material signals and drivers, assesses the need and impact of change, routes the matter through applicable decision and authorization mechanisms, implements controlled change, validates the resulting state, and feeds validated outcomes back into future governance improvement while preserving authority integrity, accountability, ownership, compliance, Platform autonomy, compatibility, and canonical consistency.

⸻

54\. Canonical Freeze Statement

H2 — Evolution Model establishes the conceptual architecture through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance evolves from a current governed state toward an authorized, validated, and improved future state using evidence, evolution drivers, assessment, applicable decision authority, controlled change, implementation, validation, and continuous feedback while preserving the constitutional boundaries established by H1.

H2 is a model, not an operating procedure, decision authority, lifecycle state machine, or implementation specification. Detailed concerns are delegated to H3–H10.

H2 is FROZEN as a Bounded Domain Reconstruction with Source Qualification.

• current policies;

• current standards;

• current controls;

• current certification mechanisms;

• current compliance mechanisms;

• current authority;

• current accountability;

• current operating model;

• current governance relationships;

• current platform interfaces;

• current evidence;

• current known limitations.

Current State harus dipahami sebelum perubahan dirancang.

Prinsip:

Do Not Evolve What Has Not Been Understood.

Assessment harus memiliki reference terhadap state yang sedang berlaku.

⸻

6\. Desired Governance State

Evolution harus memiliki tujuan yang dapat dijelaskan.

Desired State dapat berupa:

• improved governance effectiveness;

• improved compliance;

• improved accountability;

• improved traceability;

• reduced governance friction;

• improved decision quality;

• improved review effectiveness;

• improved cross-platform coordination;

• improved certification;

• improved control;

• improved adaptability;

• controlled future expansion.

Desired State tidak boleh ditentukan hanya sebagai "lebih baik".

Evolution objective harus memiliki rationale yang dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

7\. State Delta

Evolution dapat dipahami sebagai perubahan terkontrol antara Current State dan Desired State.

Current State

│

│

▼

State Delta

│

▼

Desired State

State Delta harus menjawab:

1\. apa yang berubah;

2\. mengapa berubah;

3\. siapa yang terdampak;

4\. apa yang tidak berubah;

5\. apa risiko perubahan;

6\. apa dependency perubahan;

7\. bagaimana perubahan akan divalidasi.

H2 tidak menentukan detailed impact analysis method.

Detail tersebut diturunkan kepada H6 dan H7.

⸻

8\. Evolution Signal

Tidak semua perubahan dimulai dari proposal formal.

Evolution dapat dipicu oleh signal.

Potential signals:

• governance finding;

• compliance finding;

• performance degradation;

• repeated operational friction;

• architecture review finding;

• lifecycle finding;

• integration finding;

• audit finding;

• regulatory requirement;

• policy change;

• organizational change;

• dependency change;

• risk;

• future expansion requirement.

Signal hanya merupakan input.

Signal belum merupakan authorization.

Signal

≠

Decision

⸻

9\. Evolution Driver

Evolution Driver merupakan alasan substantif mengapa Governance & Compliance perlu dipertimbangkan untuk berubah.

Canonical distinction:

Evidence

↓

Signal

↓

Driver

↓

Evolution Need

Contoh:

Repeated Compliance Failure

↓

Compliance Signal

↓

Control Weakness

↓

Evolution Driver

↓

Governance Change Candidate

H3 memiliki responsibility untuk mendefinisikan dan mengklasifikasikan Evolution Drivers.

H2 hanya menetapkan posisi Driver dalam model.

⸻

10\. Evolution Need

Evolution Driver tidak otomatis menghasilkan perubahan.

Setelah driver diidentifikasi, harus ditentukan:

Is Evolution Actually Required?

Canonical decision concept:

Evolution Driver

↓

Assessment

│

├── No Evolution Required

│

├── Operational Improvement

│

├── Lifecycle Change

│

└── Governance Evolution

Ini penting untuk mencegah semua finding berubah menjadi structural governance change.

⸻

11\. Materiality Boundary

Tidak semua improvement memiliki materiality yang sama.

Evolution model membedakan minimal:

Minor Improvement

│

▼

Material Improvement

│

▼

Structural Evolution

│

▼

Expansion

Materiality menentukan seberapa besar governance mechanism yang diperlukan.

H2 tidak menetapkan threshold numerik.

Threshold dan classification mechanism menjadi concern H4 dan H5.

⸻

12\. Evolution Path Selection

Setelah Evolution Need dinilai, perubahan harus diarahkan ke path yang sesuai.

Canonical path:

Evolution Need

│

┌─────────────┼─────────────┐

│ │ │

▼ ▼ ▼

No Change Improvement Evolution

│

▼

Expansion

Path selection tidak boleh dilakukan berdasarkan preferensi informal.

Path harus didukung oleh:

• evidence;

• materiality;

• impact;

• authority;

• ownership;

• governance requirement.

⸻

13\. Improvement Path

Jika kebutuhan tidak membutuhkan structural evolution, maka improvement dapat dilakukan tanpa mengubah governance architecture secara material.

Finding

↓

Improvement

↓

Validation

↓

Improved Current State

Improvement tetap harus berada dalam existing authority.

Improvement tidak boleh digunakan untuk secara informal mengubah constitutional boundary.

⸻

14\. Evolution Path

Jika perubahan bersifat material, evolution path digunakan.

Evolution Need

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Decision

↓

Authorization

↓

Controlled Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Validation

Evolution path digunakan untuk perubahan yang memiliki material impact terhadap Governance & Compliance.

⸻

15\. Expansion Path

Expansion merupakan bentuk evolution yang memperluas governance capability, applicability, scope, atau integration.

Expansion hanya dapat dilakukan apabila:

• existing capability sufficiently stable;

• dependency understood;

• governance impact assessed;

• ownership defined;

• authority established;

• compatibility considered.

Canonical principle:

Stability

↓

Assessment

↓

Authorization

↓

Expansion

Expansion tidak boleh digunakan untuk menghindari stabilization.

⸻

16\. Controlled Evolution Loop

Evolution harus mengikuti closed-loop model:

1\. Observe

↓

2\. Identify

↓

3\. Assess

↓

4\. Decide

↓

5\. Authorize

↓

6\. Change

↓

7\. Validate

↓

8\. Learn

↓

9\. Observe Again

Loop tersebut membentuk continuous evolution mechanism.

⸻

17\. Observe

Observe berarti mengumpulkan evidence mengenai kondisi Governance & Compliance.

Potential sources:

• governance operation;

• governance review;

• compliance review;

• performance measurement;

• architecture review;

• lifecycle review;

• integration review;

• audit;

• incident;

• decision record;

• stakeholder feedback.

Observe tidak berarti langsung mengubah governance.

⸻

18\. Identify

Identify mengubah evidence menjadi signal atau finding yang dapat dianalisis.

Contoh:

Evidence

↓

Finding

↓

Pattern

↓

Potential Evolution Driver

Pattern recognition penting karena satu anomaly tidak selalu berarti structural governance weakness.

⸻

19\. Assess

Assessment menentukan:

• materiality;

• impact;

• urgency;

• dependency;

• affected owner;

• governance implication;

• compatibility;

• risk;

• expected benefit.

Assessment menghasilkan:

Evolution Assessment

Assessment tidak sama dengan decision.

Assessment

≠

Authorization

⸻

20\. Decide

Decision menentukan apakah evolution diperlukan dan bagaimana path yang applicable.

Possible outcomes:

No Change

Improvement

Controlled Evolution

Expansion

Escalation

Decision harus menggunakan authority yang berlaku.

H5 bertanggung jawab terhadap detailed Evolution Decision Governance.

⸻

21\. Authorize

Authorization memastikan perubahan memiliki authority yang sah.

H2 hanya menempatkan authorization dalam model.

H2 tidak menetapkan siapa final authority secara baru.

Authority berasal dari:

• Governance Constitution;

• Authority Governance;

• applicable decision governance;

• delegated authority;

• higher-level authority apabila diperlukan.

⸻

22\. Change

Change merupakan actual modification terhadap governance state.

Potential change objects:

• policy;

• standard;

• control;

• certification mechanism;

• compliance mechanism;

• governance relationship;

• governance process;

• governance capability;

• governance artifact.

Change harus controlled.

Minimum major-change discipline:

Reason

-

Impact Analysis

-

Owner

-

Approval

-

Version

⸻

23\. Implementation

Implementation menerjemahkan authorized change menjadi actual changed state.

Authorized Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Changed Governance State

Implementation tidak boleh mengubah scope authorization secara unilateral.

H8 bertanggung jawab terhadap detailed Evolution Implementation.

⸻

24\. Validation

Setelah implementation, perubahan harus divalidasi.

Validation menjawab:

• apakah intended change terjadi;

• apakah governance requirement terpenuhi;

• apakah compatibility tetap terjaga;

• apakah ownership tetap jelas;

• apakah unintended impact muncul;

• apakah expected outcome tercapai.

Implementation

↓

Validation

│

├── PASS

│

└── FAIL

Failure menghasilkan:

Rework

Rollback

Mitigation

Further Evolution

Escalation

sesuai authority dan control yang berlaku.

⸻

25\. New Governance State

Jika validation berhasil, system memasuki new governance state.

Old State

↓

Authorized Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Validation

↓

New State

New State harus menjadi canonical reference apabila perubahan memang telah disahkan dan dibekukan sesuai applicable governance process.

⸻

26\. Learning Loop

New State menghasilkan evidence baru.

New Governance State

↓

Operation

↓

Evidence

↓

Review

↓

Learning

↓

Potential New Driver

Dengan demikian evolution tidak berakhir pada implementation.

Evolution berakhir pada:

validated state + captured learning

yang kemudian menjadi input bagi future evolution.

⸻

Performance Architecture menyediakan measurement dan evidence.

Relationship:

Performance

↓

Measurement

↓

Finding / Signal

↓

H2 tidak memiliki ownership terhadap performance measurement.

Performance architecture tetap bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• measurement;

• metrics;

• performance evidence;

• performance review.

Evolution menggunakan output tersebut sebagai input.

Governance Operations

32\. Relationship to Platform Autonomy

Evolution model harus mempertahankan:

Each Platform Owns Its Domain.

Governance evolution tidak otomatis menghasilkan ownership transfer.

PD-03

Governance Requirement

↓

↓

Domain Owner

↓

Execution / Remediation

Dengan demikian:

Governance Evolution

≠

33\. Evolution Boundary Preservation

Setiap evolution cycle wajib memeriksa:

Authority

Apakah authority tetap valid?

Ownership

Apakah owner tetap jelas?

Accountability

Apakah accountability tetap dapat ditelusuri?

Compliance

Apakah compliance boundary tetap terpenuhi?

Platform Autonomy

Apakah domain Platform lain tetap autonomous?

Architecture

Apakah architecture boundary berubah?

Lifecycle

Apakah lifecycle implication muncul?

Integration

Apakah cross-platform dependency berubah?

⸻

34\. Compatibility Model

Evolution harus mempertimbangkan compatibility.

Canonical model:

Existing State

↓

Compatibility Assessment

│

├── Compatible

│ ↓

│ Controlled Adoption

│

└── Incompatible

↓

Migration

Jika compatibility tidak dapat dipertahankan, migration atau mitigation harus dirancang.

Backward compatibility merupakan evolution principle yang didukung source.

⸻

35\. Canonical Reference Model

Evolution harus dimulai dari canonical reference yang berlaku.

Reference precedence:

Executive Decision

↓

Volume 0

↓

Volume Platform

↓

Supporting Documents

Supporting document tidak boleh secara informal mengalahkan canonical authority.

Prinsip:

Canonical First

merupakan bagian dari evolution principles yang didukung source.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

36\. Evolution Stability Model

Evolution tidak boleh dilakukan hanya karena tersedia kemampuan untuk berubah.

Canonical principle:

Stable Current State

↓

Understand Need

↓

Assess Impact

↓

Controlled Change

Jika current state belum stabil:

Unstable State

↓

Stabilization

↓

Reassessment

↓

Evolution

Prinsip Stability Before Expansion berlaku terutama untuk expansion.

⸻

37\. Evolution Scope Classification

H2 menggunakan classification berikut sebagai conceptual model:

Class Meaning

No Change Tidak diperlukan perubahan

Improvement Perbaikan tanpa structural evolution

Material Evolution Perubahan governance yang material

Structural Evolution Perubahan architecture/capability yang signifikan

Expansion Perluasan governance capability atau applicability

Classification ini bersifat architectural model.

Threshold formal harus ditentukan oleh section berikutnya apabila source atau architecture decision menetapkannya.

⸻

38\. Evolution Decision Matrix

Conceptual decision matrix:

Condition Preferred Path

Evidence tidak menunjukkan material issue No Change

Issue lokal dan bounded Improvement

Governance mechanism perlu berubah Material Evolution

Structural relationship berubah Structural Evolution

Capability/scope diperluas Expansion

Authority ambiguity Governance Review / Escalation

Cross-platform impact tinggi Cross-Platform Review

Compatibility tidak dapat dipertahankan Migration Path

Matrix ini tidak menggantikan authority decision.

⸻

39\. Evolution Risk Model

Evolution harus mempertimbangkan risk.

Potential risks:

• authority conflict;

• ownership ambiguity;

• governance regression;

• compliance regression;

• platform disruption;

• integration breakage;

• lifecycle inconsistency;

• documentation drift;

• canonical divergence;

• uncontrolled expansion.

Risk assessment harus menjadi input terhadap decision dan control.

⸻

40\. Evolution Reversibility

Jika memungkinkan, evolution harus mempertimbangkan reversibility.

Reversible

↓

Controlled Rollout

↓

Validation

atau:

↓

Higher Impact Assessment

↓

Higher Governance Assurance

↓

Explicit Authorization

H2 tidak menetapkan rollback mechanism secara detail.

Rollback dan control menjadi concern H7/H8.

45.1 Change Without Evidence

Preference

Change

Need

46\. Evolution Model Invariants

Invariant berikut harus selalu dipertahankan:

Invariant 1

Evolution must have a reason.

Invariant 2

Evolution must remain within authority.

Invariant 3

Evolution must preserve ownership clarity.

Invariant 4

Evolution must preserve Platform autonomy.

Invariant 5

Material change must be controlled.

Invariant 6

Evolution must be traceable.

Invariant 7

Evolution must be validated.

Invariant 8

Expansion follows sufficient stability.

Invariant 9

Canonical authority remains authoritative.

Invariant 10

Evolution does not automatically create new authority.

50\. Evolution Model Canonical Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance evolves through a controlled, evidence-based, authority-bounded feedback model that observes the current governance state, identifies material signals and drivers, assesses the need and impact of change, routes the matter through applicable decision and authorization mechanisms, implements controlled change, validates the resulting state, and feeds validated outcomes back into future governance improvement while preserving authority integrity, accountability, ownership, compliance, Platform autonomy, compatibility, and canonical consistency.

⸻

54\. Canonical Freeze Statement

H2 — Evolution Model establishes the conceptual architecture through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance evolves from a current governed state toward an authorized, validated, and improved future state using evidence, evolution drivers, assessment, applicable decision authority, controlled change, implementation, validation, and continuous feedback while preserving the constitutional boundaries established by H1.

H2 is a model, not an operating procedure, decision authority, lifecycle state machine, or implementation specification. Detailed concerns are delegated to H3–H10.

H2 is FROZEN as a Bounded Domain Reconstruction with Source Qualification.

\# 1. Purpose

H3 mendefinisikan sumber dan kondisi yang dapat menjadi

\*\*driver bagi Governance Evolution\*\* pada PD-03.

H3 menjawab:

\> \*\*Mengapa Governance & Compliance perlu dipertimbangkan

\> untuk berevolusi?\*\*

H3 tidak menentukan:

\- apakah evolution harus disetujui;

\- siapa yang memberikan authorization;

\- bagaimana perubahan dirancang;

\- bagaimana perubahan diimplementasikan;

\- bagaimana evolution dikontrol;

\- kapan evolution dinyatakan berhasil.

Concern tersebut didelegasikan kepada:

\`\`\`text

H4 — Evolution Change Architecture

H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

H6 — Evolution Review

H7 — Evolution Control

H8 — Evolution Implementation

H9 — Evolution Improvement & Expansion

H10 — Evolution Success

2\. Constitutional Position

H3 berada di bawah:

H1 — Evolution Constitution

↓

H2 — Evolution Model

↓

H3 — Evolution Drivers

H1 menetapkan constitutional boundary.

H2 menetapkan evolution model.

H3 mengidentifikasi driver yang dapat memasukkan matter ke dalam evolution assessment.

Canonical distinction:

Evidence

↓

Signal / Finding

↓

Evolution Driver

↓

Evolution Need

↓

Assessment

Driver bukan decision.

Driver bukan authorization.

Driver bukan change.

⸻

3\. Definition

Evolution Driver adalah kondisi, finding, requirement, risk, evidence pattern, atau perubahan lingkungan yang memberikan legitimate reason untuk mempertimbangkan perubahan terhadap Governance & Compliance.

Driver harus menjelaskan:

• apa yang berubah;

• kondisi apa yang diamati;

• mengapa kondisi tersebut relevan;

• governance concern apa yang terdampak;

• mengapa existing state mungkin tidak lagi memadai.

Canonical principle:

Driver

\=

Reason to Assess Evolution

Bukan:

Driver

\=

Automatic Authorization to Change

⸻

4\. Driver Qualification

Sebuah kondisi tidak boleh langsung diperlakukan sebagai Evolution Driver hanya karena terdapat anomaly atau ketidaknyamanan.

Minimum qualification:

Observed Condition

↓

Relevant to Governance

↓

Material / Persistent / Required

↓

Legitimate Driver

Driver harus memiliki evidence atau rationale yang dapat ditelusuri.

Source mendukung bahwa governance evolution harus memiliki legitimate driver dan evidence/rationale yang traceable.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

5\. Driver Sources

Driver dapat berasal dari beberapa sumber.

Canonical source families:

Governance

Compliance

Performance

Lifecycle

Integration

Architecture

Audit

Operations

External Requirement

Future Requirement

Sumber-sumber tersebut merupakan driver families, bukan automatic change categories.

⸻

6\. Governance Findings

Governance finding merupakan salah satu sumber utama Evolution Driver.

Contoh:

• governance mechanism tidak efektif;

• accountability ambiguity;

• authority ambiguity;

• review weakness;

• delegation weakness;

• escalation weakness;

• governance control weakness;

• governance traceability gap;

• policy inconsistency;

• standards inconsistency.

Model:

Governance Finding

↓

Pattern / Materiality

↓

Evolution Driver

Governance finding tidak otomatis berarti structural evolution diperlukan.

⸻

7\. Compliance Findings

Compliance finding dapat menjadi Evolution Driver ketika finding menunjukkan bahwa governance mechanism yang ada tidak lagi memadai.

Contoh:

• recurring compliance failure;

• systemic control weakness;

• repeated exception;

• incomplete evidence;

• inconsistent compliance interpretation;

• ineffective corrective action;

• certification weakness.

Governance compliance source menetapkan bahwa findings, corrective action, validation, dan improvement menjadi bagian dari governance compliance lifecycle.

Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang .txt

Model:

Compliance Requirement

↓

Assessment

↓

Finding

↓

Corrective Action

↓

Pattern / Systemic Concern

↓

Evolution Driver

⸻

8\. Performance Evidence

Performance evidence dapat menjadi input bagi Evolution Driver.

Performance tidak sama dengan evolution.

Performance

↓

Evidence

↓

Finding / Signal

↓

Potential Evolution Driver

Performance evidence dapat menunjukkan:

• governance friction;

• repeated control failure;

• review inefficiency;

• decision latency;

• compliance degradation;

• governance effectiveness gap.

Namun performance failure tidak boleh langsung dianggap sebagai governance evolution requirement.

Cause harus terlebih dahulu dibedakan.

Source menegaskan:

Performance memberikan evidence; evolution menentukan apakah governance perlu berubah.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

Lifecycle Architecture dapat menghasilkan evidence yang menjadi Evolution Driver.

Contoh:

• lifecycle transition menghasilkan governance gap;

• lifecycle state tidak lagi sesuai dengan governance mechanism;

• lifecycle change menghasilkan compliance implication;

• governance requirement tidak lagi sesuai dengan lifecycle condition.

Model:

Lifecycle Evidence

↓

Lifecycle Finding

↓

Governance Implication

↓

Potential Evolution Driver

• integration contract menghasilkan governance implication.

Governance Implication

• architecture review menemukan governance boundary gap.

Governance Impact

12\. Audit Findings

Audit dapat menghasilkan evidence mengenai governance effectiveness atau compliance.

Audit finding dapat menjadi driver apabila:

• finding berulang;

• finding menunjukkan systemic weakness;

• current governance mechanism tidak memadai;

• corrective action memerlukan governance evolution.

Audit Finding

• excessive governance handoff;

• recurring documentation gap;

• repeated review ambiguity.

≠

30\. Driver Evidence Quality

Investigate

⸻

31\. Driver Legitimacy

Driver dianggap legitimate apabila:

0\. berada dalam governance concern;

0\. memiliki evidence atau rationale;

0\. relevan terhadap current governance state;

0\. dapat ditelusuri;

0\. memiliki sufficient materiality atau mandatory basis.

Tidak legitimate:

Personal Preference

Convenience

Undocumented Assumption

Political Preference

Unverified Claim

Source secara eksplisit melarang evolution hanya berdasarkan preference, convenience, undocumented assumption, dan organizational politics.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

42\. Driver Rejection

Driver dapat ditolak apabila:

• evidence tidak cukup;

• tidak relevan dengan governance;

• duplicate;

• already resolved;

• tidak material;

• berada di luar authority domain;

• tidak ada legitimate requirement.

Rejection harus tetap traceable untuk material driver.

⸻

43\. Driver Closure

Driver dapat ditutup apabila:

• issue resolved;

• requirement withdrawn;

• duplicate consolidated;

• assessment concludes no evolution required;

• concern transferred ke applicable domain owner;

• driver tidak lagi valid.

Closure tidak sama dengan evolution success.

⸻

44\. Anti-Patterns

H3 melarang:

44.1 Driver = Decision

Driver

↓

Automatic Change

Tidak diperbolehkan.

44.2 Finding = Structural Evolution

Tidak semua finding membutuhkan structural evolution.

44.3 Performance Failure = Governance Failure

Cause harus dibedakan.

44.4 External Requirement = Automatic Internal Design

Applicability harus dinilai.

44.5 Driver = Authority

Driver tidak menciptakan authority.

44.6 Driver = Ownership

Driver tidak mentransfer ownership.

44.7 Unverified Claim = Driver

Evidence atau rationale yang sah diperlukan.

44.8 Every Friction = Evolution

Friction harus dinilai materiality dan persistence-nya.

53\. Canonical Freeze Statement

H3 — Evolution Drivers defines the legitimate sources and conditions that may cause PD-03 Governance & Compliance to consider evolution, using traceable evidence, findings, requirements, risks, operational signals, and environmental changes while preserving the distinction between evidence, driver, evolution need, decision, authorization, change, and execution ownership.

H3 does not authorize change. H3 does not define change architecture. H3 qualifies the reason for entering the evolution assessment path.

H3 is FROZEN as a Bounded Domain Reconstruction with Source Qualification.

⸻

54\. Construction Boundary

H3 must not be used to infer:

• automatic authorization;

• exact approval authority;

• mandatory change;

• exact materiality thresholds;

• exact risk thresholds;

• exact review cadence;

• implementation workflow;

• technical ownership;

• operational ownership;

• cross-platform execution authority.

Those concerns belong to the applicable downstream architecture and governance sections.

\# 1. Purpose

H3 mendefinisikan sumber dan kondisi yang dapat menjadi

\*\*driver bagi Governance Evolution\*\* pada PD-03.

H3 menjawab:

\> \*\*Mengapa Governance & Compliance perlu dipertimbangkan

\> untuk berevolusi?\*\*

H3 tidak menentukan:

\- apakah evolution harus disetujui;

\- siapa yang memberikan authorization;

\- bagaimana perubahan dirancang;

\- bagaimana perubahan diimplementasikan;

\- bagaimana evolution dikontrol;

\- kapan evolution dinyatakan berhasil.

Evidence

↓

Signal / Finding

↓

Evolution Driver

↓

Evolution Need

Assessment

Driver bukan decision.

Driver bukan authorization.

⸻

3\. Definition

Evolution Driver adalah kondisi, finding, requirement, risk, evidence pattern, atau perubahan lingkungan yang memberikan legitimate reason untuk mempertimbangkan perubahan terhadap Governance & Compliance.

Driver harus menjelaskan:

• apa yang berubah;

• kondisi apa yang diamati;

• mengapa kondisi tersebut relevan;

• governance concern apa yang terdampak;

• mengapa existing state mungkin tidak lagi memadai.

Canonical principle:

Driver

\=

Reason to Assess Evolution

Bukan:

Driver

\=

Automatic Authorization to Change

⸻

4\. Driver Qualification

Sebuah kondisi tidak boleh langsung diperlakukan sebagai Evolution Driver hanya karena terdapat anomaly atau ketidaknyamanan.

Minimum qualification:

Observed Condition

↓

Relevant to Governance

↓

Material / Persistent / Required

↓

Legitimate Driver

Driver harus memiliki evidence atau rationale yang dapat ditelusuri.

Source mendukung bahwa governance evolution harus memiliki legitimate driver dan evidence/rationale yang traceable.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

Audit

Operations

External Requirement

Future Requirement

Sumber-sumber tersebut merupakan driver families, bukan automatic change categories.

⸻

6\. Governance Findings

Governance finding merupakan salah satu sumber utama Evolution Driver.

Contoh:

• governance mechanism tidak efektif;

• accountability ambiguity;

• authority ambiguity;

• review weakness;

• delegation weakness;

• escalation weakness;

• governance control weakness;

• governance traceability gap;

• policy inconsistency;

• standards inconsistency.

Model:

Governance Finding

↓

Pattern / Materiality

↓

Evolution Driver

Governance finding tidak otomatis berarti structural evolution diperlukan.

⸻

7\. Compliance Findings

Compliance finding dapat menjadi Evolution Driver ketika finding menunjukkan bahwa governance mechanism yang ada tidak lagi memadai.

Contoh:

• recurring compliance failure;

• systemic control weakness;

• repeated exception;

• incomplete evidence;

• inconsistent compliance interpretation;

• ineffective corrective action;

• certification weakness.

Governance compliance source menetapkan bahwa findings, corrective action, validation, dan improvement menjadi bagian dari governance compliance lifecycle.

Assessment

Finding

Performance

↓

Evidence

↓

Finding / Signal

↓

Potential Evolution Driver

Performance evidence dapat menunjukkan:

• governance friction;

• repeated control failure;

• review inefficiency;

• decision latency;

• compliance degradation;

• governance effectiveness gap.

Namun performance failure tidak boleh langsung dianggap sebagai governance evolution requirement.

Cause harus terlebih dahulu dibedakan.

Source menegaskan:

Performance memberikan evidence; evolution menentukan apakah governance perlu berubah.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

• lifecycle change menghasilkan compliance implication;

• governance requirement tidak lagi sesuai dengan lifecycle condition.

Lifecycle Finding

28\. Driver Materiality

Driver harus diklasifikasikan menurut materiality.

Conceptual classes:

Class Meaning

Informational Tidak membutuhkan response

Observational Perlu monitoring

Improvement Signal Memerlukan improvement assessment

Material Driver Memerlukan formal evolution assessment

Structural Driver Berpotensi memerlukan structural evolution

Expansion Driver Berpotensi memerlukan capability/scope expansion

30\. Driver Evidence Quality

Driver harus mempertimbangkan kualitas evidence.

31\. Driver Legitimacy

Driver dianggap legitimate apabila:

1\. berada dalam governance concern;

2\. memiliki evidence atau rationale;

3\. relevan terhadap current governance state;

4\. dapat ditelusuri;

5\. memiliki sufficient materiality atau mandatory basis.

Tidak legitimate:

Personal Preference

Convenience

Undocumented Assumption

Political Preference

Unverified Claim

Source secara eksplisit melarang evolution hanya berdasarkan preference, convenience, undocumented assumption, dan organizational politics.

42\. Driver Rejection

Driver dapat ditolak apabila:

• evidence tidak cukup;

• tidak relevan dengan governance;

• duplicate;

• already resolved;

• tidak material;

• berada di luar authority domain;

• tidak ada legitimate requirement.

Rejection harus tetap traceable untuk material driver.

⸻

43\. Driver Closure

Driver dapat ditutup apabila:

• issue resolved;

• requirement withdrawn;

• duplicate consolidated;

• assessment concludes no evolution required;

• concern transferred ke applicable domain owner;

• driver tidak lagi valid.

Closure tidak sama dengan evolution success.

44\. Anti-Patterns

H3 melarang:

44.1 Driver = Decision

Driver

↓

Automatic Change

Tidak diperbolehkan.

44.2 Finding = Structural Evolution

Tidak semua finding membutuhkan structural evolution.

44.3 Performance Failure = Governance Failure

Cause harus dibedakan.

44.4 External Requirement = Automatic Internal Design

Applicability harus dinilai.

44.5 Driver = Authority

Driver tidak menciptakan authority.

44.6 Driver = Ownership

Driver tidak mentransfer ownership.

44.7 Unverified Claim = Driver

Evidence atau rationale yang sah diperlukan.

44.8 Every Friction = Evolution

Friction harus dinilai materiality dan persistence-nya.

53\. Canonical Freeze Statement

H3 — Evolution Drivers defines the legitimate sources and conditions that may cause PD-03 Governance & Compliance to consider evolution, using traceable evidence, findings, requirements, risks, operational signals, and environmental changes while preserving the distinction between evidence, driver, evolution need, decision, authorization, change, and execution ownership.

H3 does not authorize change. H3 does not define change architecture. H3 qualifies the reason for entering the evolution assessment path.

H3 is FROZEN as a Bounded Domain Reconstruction with Source Qualification.

⸻

54\. Construction Boundary

H3 must not be used to infer:

• automatic authorization;

• exact approval authority;

• mandatory change;

• exact materiality thresholds;

• exact risk thresholds;

• exact review cadence;

• implementation workflow;

• technical ownership;

• operational ownership;

• cross-platform execution authority.

Those concerns belong to the applicable downstream architecture and governance sections.

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# 1. Purpose

H4 mendefinisikan bagaimana \*\*qualified Evolution Driver\*\*

diterjemahkan menjadi \*\*controlled governance change

candidate\*\* dalam domain PD-03.

H4 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana kebutuhan evolution dibentuk menjadi perubahan

\> yang dapat dianalisis, dimiliki, disetujui, dikontrol, dan

\> ditelusuri?\*\*

H4 berada di antara:

\`\`\`text

H3 — Evolution Drivers

↓

H4 — Evolution Change Architecture

↓

H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

H4 tidak memberikan authorization.

H4 tidak melakukan implementation.

H4 membentuk change architecture yang siap masuk ke decision governance.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

Relationship:

H1

Evolution Constitution

↓

H2

Evolution Model

↓

H3

Evolution Drivers

↓

Evolution Change Architecture

↓

H4 harus tetap mematuhi constitutional principles:

• legitimate reason;

• evidence;

• impact awareness;

• ownership;

• authority;

• review;

• controlled change;

• traceability;

• compatibility;

• Platform autonomy.

Baseline menyatakan bahwa evolution harus memiliki legitimate reason, identifiable driver, impact analysis, owner, appropriate authority, applicable review, traceable decision, governance integrity, Platform autonomy, dan evidence.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

3\. Definition

Evolution Change Architecture adalah struktur yang mengubah Evolution Need menjadi defined change candidate yang memiliki:

• reason;

• scope;

• target state;

• affected state;

• impact;

• owner;

• dependencies;

• compatibility consideration;

• migration consideration;

• approval requirement;

• version identity;

• traceability.

Canonical distinction:

Evolution Driver

↓

Why change is considered

Change Architecture

↓

What change is being proposed

-

How its impact and boundaries are represented

H4 tidak menentukan apakah change akan disetujui.

⸻

Baseline secara eksplisit menetapkan untuk major change:

Change Reason

↓

Impact Analysis

↓

Owner

↓

Approval

↓

Versioned Change

Minimum requirements:

• reason for change;

• impact analysis;

• owner;

• approval;

• versioning.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa backward compatibility harus dipertahankan sejauh memungkinkan, dan jika compatibility tidak dapat dipertahankan maka migration mechanism harus tersedia dan traceable.

⸻

8\. Current State

Setiap material change harus memiliki pemahaman terhadap state yang sedang berlaku.

Minimum representation:

Current State

↓

Existing Governance Condition

Current state dapat mencakup:

• applicable policy;

• applicable standard;

• existing control;

• existing certification mechanism;

• existing compliance mechanism;

• existing authority;

• existing ownership;

• existing dependency;

• existing governance relationship.

Current state menjadi baseline untuk impact analysis.

⸻

9\. Target State

Setiap material change harus mendefinisikan intended target state.

Current State

↓

Proposed Change

↓

Target State

Target state harus cukup jelas untuk menjawab:

• apa yang berubah;

• apa yang tetap;

• governance mechanism apa yang ditambahkan;

• governance mechanism apa yang diubah;

• governance mechanism apa yang retired;

• relationship apa yang berubah.

Target state bukan implementation plan.

⸻

10\. Change Objective

Setiap change harus memiliki objective yang eksplisit.

Canonical pattern:

Driver

↓

Problem / Opportunity

↓

Change Objective

↓

Target State

Objective harus menjelaskan outcome yang ingin dicapai.

Tidak diperbolehkan menggunakan:

• vague objective;

• undocumented preference;

• convenience-only rationale.

⸻

11\. Change Reason

Reason for change merupakan mandatory foundation untuk material change.

Reason harus menjelaskan:

• driver;

• context;

• governance concern;

• why existing state is insufficient;

• expected purpose of change.

Baseline secara eksplisit menetapkan reason for change sebagai minimum requirement major change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

12\. Change Scope

Change scope harus menentukan:

Included

Apa yang berubah.

Excluded

Apa yang tidak berubah.

Affected

Domain atau governance relationship yang terdampak.

Model:

Change

├── In Scope

├── Out of Scope

└── Affected

Scope harus mencegah change creep.

⸻

13\. Change Boundary

Change boundary harus menjaga:

• PD-03 governance boundary;

• Architecture Authority;

• operational ownership;

• Platform autonomy;

• organizational ownership.

Canonical rule:

Governance Change

≠

Automatic Domain Ownership Change

Governance requirement dapat memiliki impact terhadap domain lain tanpa mengambil alih ownership domain tersebut.

⸻

14\. Change Classification

Materiality perlu dipertimbangkan ketika membentuk change.

Conceptual classification:

￼

Source qualification: exact classification taxonomy di atas tidak ditemukan sebagai taxonomy literal dalam baseline.

Classification ini merupakan bounded reconstruction.

Formal threshold harus ditetapkan oleh authority yang berlaku, bukan oleh H4 secara sepihak.

⸻

15\. Major Change

Major governance evolution wajib diperlakukan sebagai controlled change.

Minimum source-supported requirements:

Reason

-

Impact Analysis

-

Owner

-

Approval

-

Versioning

Performance Architecture Review.txt

H4 merepresentasikan requirement tersebut.

H5 menentukan applicable decision and approval governance.

16\. Change Object

Material change harus memiliki identifiable change object.

Conceptual model:

Change Object

├── Change ID

├── Reason

├── Driver

├── Current State

├── Target State

├── Scope

├── Impact

├── Dependencies

├── Owner

├── Authority Requirement

├── Approval Requirement

├── Compatibility

├── Migration

├── Version

├── Status

└── Traceability

Schema ini merupakan bounded reconstruction.

⸻

17\. Change Identity

Setiap material change harus memiliki identity yang stabil.

Minimum conceptual identity:

Change ID

Version

Status

Change identity diperlukan agar:

• review dapat merujuk change yang tepat;

• implementation dapat ditelusuri;

• version history dapat dipertahankan;

• superseded change dapat dibedakan dari active change.

⸻

18\. Change Versioning

Versioning merupakan explicit source-supported requirement untuk major change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Versioning harus membedakan:

Change Proposal

↓

Reviewed Change

↓

Approved Change

↓

Implemented Change

↓

Validated Change

Versioning tidak berarti setiap minor edit harus menjadi major version.

Exact versioning convention berada pada applicable documentation/change-control mechanism.

⸻

19\. Change State

Change state harus dapat dibedakan dari lifecycle state.

Conceptual change states:

Draft

↓

Defined

↓

Assessed

↓

Reviewable

↓

Approved

↓

Controlled

↓

Implemented

↓

Validated

↓

Closed

Status taxonomy di atas adalah bounded reconstruction.

H4 tidak memberikan status Approved tanpa H5 decision.

20\. Change Dependency

Change harus mengidentifikasi dependency yang relevan.

Dependency dapat mencakup:

• governance dependency;

• architecture dependency;

• lifecycle dependency;

• performance dependency;

• platform dependency;

• compliance dependency;

• organizational dependency.

Model:

Change

│

├── Dependency A

├── Dependency B

└── Dependency C

Dependency tidak sama dengan ownership.

21\. Cross-Platform Change

Jika governance evolution memengaruhi Platform lain:

PD-03 Change

↓

Cross-Platform Impact

↓

Applicable Platform Owners

↓

Coordination

↓

Applicable Authority

PD-03 tidak boleh menggunakan governance evolution untuk mengambil alih execution domain Platform lain.

Cross-platform impact harus ditangani melalui applicable coordination mechanism.

⸻

22\. Impact Architecture

Impact Analysis merupakan explicit source-supported requirement untuk major change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

H4 harus menyediakan struktur untuk impact analysis.

Minimum dimensions:

Governance Impact

Architecture Impact

Organization Impact

Lifecycle Impact

Performance Impact

Compliance Impact

Integration Impact

Operational Impact

Platform Impact

Tidak semua dimension wajib memiliki material impact.

Yang penting adalah impact yang relevan dapat terlihat.

23\. Governance Impact

Assess:

• policy impact;

• standard impact;

• control impact;

• certification impact;

• compliance impact;

• approval impact;

• accountability impact;

• authority impact.

Model:

Change

↓

Governance Impact

├── Standards

├── Control

├── Certification

└── Compliance

Governance Change

Architecture Impact

Applicable Architecture Governance

25\. Organization Impact

Assess potential impact terhadap:

• responsibility;

• accountability;

• role;

• reporting;

• ownership;

• organizational structure.

Governance change tidak otomatis mengubah organization architecture.

Jika organization change diperlukan, gunakan applicable organizational mechanism.

26\. Lifecycle Impact

Change harus mempertimbangkan apakah governance evolution mempengaruhi lifecycle condition.

Contoh:

Governance Change

↓

Lifecycle Implication

↓

H4 tidak mengambil alih Lifecycle Authority.

27\. Performance Impact

Change dapat memiliki performance implication.

Contoh:

• review overhead;

• compliance workload;

• governance efficiency;

• decision latency.

Performance impact menjadi input bagi applicable Performance Architecture.

H4 tidak menetapkan performance target.

Assess:

• requirement alignment;

• evidence requirement;

• control obligation;

• compliance risk;

Compliance impact harus dapat ditelusuri kepada applicable requirement.

30\. Operational Impact

Governance change dapat menghasilkan:

• new process;

• additional control;

• new review;

• new evidence requirement;

• new approval step;

• changed operating burden.

Operational owner tetap bertanggung jawab atas execution.

31\. Compatibility Architecture

Governance evolution harus mempertimbangkan backward compatibility.

Compatibility dapat dipertimbangkan terhadap:

• policy;

• platform relationship;

33\. Migration Architecture

Jika compatibility tidak dapat dipertahankan, migration mechanism harus tersedia dan dapat ditelusuri.

35\. Rollback / Reversal Consideration

Material change harus mempertimbangkan consequence jika target state tidak dapat dipertahankan.

Conceptual model:

Approved Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Failure / Regression

↓

Mitigation / Reversal / Migration

Exact rollback mechanism bukan H4 authority.

Jika rollback tidak feasible, alternative mitigation atau migration harus dapat diidentifikasi.

⸻

36\. Non-Regression

Change architecture harus mengidentifikasi potential regression.

Minimum concern:

• governance integrity;

• authority clarity;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• compliance;

• traceability;

• Platform autonomy;

• canonical consistency.

Jika regression tidak dapat dihindari, rationale, impact assessment, authority decision, migration or mitigation, dan consequence harus terdokumentasi.

⸻

37\. Ownership Architecture

Owner merupakan explicit source-supported requirement untuk major change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Change architecture harus dapat menjawab:

Who owns the change?

Ownership harus dibedakan:

￼

H4 tidak mengubah ownership hanya karena change memiliki cross-platform impact.

38\. Authority Requirement

Change architecture harus merepresentasikan authority yang dibutuhkan.

Canonical distinction:

Authority Requirement

≠

Authority Decision

authority apa yang dibutuhkan?

H5 menjawab:

apakah authority tersebut memberikan decision?

H4 tidak menciptakan authority baru.

39\. Approval Requirement

Approval requirement merupakan source-supported requirement untuk major change.

Change

≠

40\. Reviewability

Change architecture harus membuat change cukup jelas untuk review.

• target state;

• impact;

• owner;

• dependencies;

• compatibility;

• migration;

• required authority.

Change yang tidak cukup defined tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai review-ready material change.

49\. Change State Integrity

Change state harus konsisten dengan decision state.

Tidak diperbolehkan:

Change Status = Approved

Decision Record = Missing

atau:

Change Status = Implemented

Approval = Missing

untuk material controlled change.

State inconsistency harus menjadi governance finding.

⸻

50\. Change Conflict

Jika change candidate bertentangan dengan:

• canonical architecture;

• governance policy;

• standards;

• executive decision;

• existing authority;

• existing ownership;

maka conflict harus diidentifikasi sebelum approval.

Canonical-first principle berlaku.

Supporting documentation tidak boleh secara informal mengalahkan canonical authority.

Sebelum membuat change baru:

Search Existing Change

↓

Search Existing Decision

↓

Search Existing Governance Mechanism

↓

Determine Reuse / Adaptation

↓

Create New Change if Justified

Ini mengikuti prinsip Reference Before Duplication.

Superseded

↓

Supersession bukan deletion.

53\. Change Retirement

Change object dapat retired apabila:

• superseded;

• cancelled;

• rejected;

• obsolete;

• requirement withdrawn.

Retirement harus mempertahankan historical traceability.

⸻

54\. Change Cancellation

Change dapat dibatalkan apabila:

• driver no longer valid;

• requirement withdrawn;

• impact unacceptable;

• duplicate;

• better alternative exists;

• authority rejects proposal.

Cancellation harus memiliki reason.

⸻

Rejection merupakan decision outcome, bukan H4 decision.

H4 hanya menyediakan structure agar rejection dapat ditelusuri terhadap:

• evidence;

• impact;

• decision.

Exact option-selection mechanism berada pada H5.

61\. Change Architecture Quality Criteria

H4 dianggap structurally sound apabila:

0\. change memiliki legitimate reason;

0\. driver dapat ditelusuri;

0\. current state jelas;

0\. target state jelas;

0\. scope bounded;

0\. impact dapat dianalisis;

0\. owner jelas;

0\. dependencies terlihat;

0\. compatibility dipertimbangkan;

0\. migration disiapkan jika diperlukan;

0\. authority requirement terlihat;

0\. approval requirement terlihat;

0\. versioning tersedia;

0\. traceability tersedia;

0\. Platform autonomy terjaga.

⸻

62\. Anti-Patterns

H4 melarang:

62.1 Change Without Driver

Preference

↓

Change

Tidak legitimate.

62.2 Change Without Impact

Material change tidak boleh masuk approval tanpa impact consideration.

62.3 Change Without Owner

Material change harus memiliki owner.

62.4 Change Without Approval Path

Major change harus memiliki applicable approval requirement.

62.5 Approval as Architecture

Change definition bukan approval.

62.6 Documentation as Authority

Dokumentasi tidak otomatis menjadi authority.

62.7 Implementation as Authorization

Implementasi tidak membuktikan authorization.

62.8 Dependency as Ownership

Cross-platform impact tidak mentransfer ownership.

62.9 Compatibility Ignored

Breaking change tanpa migration consideration tidak memenuhi controlled evolution principle.

62.10 Silent Drift

Material change tidak boleh terjadi tanpa traceable change record.

74\. Canonical Freeze Statement

H4 — Evolution Change Architecture defines the bounded structure through which a qualified Evolution Driver is translated into a reviewable and traceable governance change candidate, including reason, scope, current state, target state, impact, ownership, dependencies, compatibility, migration, authority requirement, approval requirement, and version identity.

H4 does not authorize change, execute change, or determine change success. It prepares the change architecture for applicable decision governance, control, implementation, and validation.

H4 is FROZEN as a Bounded Domain Reconstruction with Source Qualification.

⸻

75\. Construction Boundary

H4 must not be used to infer:

• automatic approval;

• new authority;

• ownership transfer;

• implementation authority;

• mandatory technical architecture;

• operational command;

• final compliance acceptance;

• final success.

Those concerns remain under the applicable authority and downstream Part H sections.

H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

H4 tidak memberikan authorization.

H4 tidak melakukan implementation.

H4 membentuk change architecture yang siap masuk ke decision governance.

⸻

2\. Constitutional Position

Relationship:

H1

Evolution Constitution

↓

H2

Evolution Model

↓

H3

Evolution Drivers

↓

H4

Evolution Change Architecture

↓

H5

Evolution Decision Governance

H4 harus tetap mematuhi constitutional principles:

• legitimate reason;

• evidence;

• impact awareness;

• ownership;

• authority;

• review;

• controlled change;

• traceability;

• compatibility;

• Platform autonomy.

Baseline menyatakan bahwa evolution harus memiliki legitimate reason, identifiable driver, impact analysis, owner, appropriate authority, applicable review, traceable decision, governance integrity, Platform autonomy, dan evidence.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

3\. Definition

Evolution Change Architecture adalah struktur yang mengubah Evolution Need menjadi defined change candidate yang memiliki:

• reason;

• scope;

• target state;

• affected state;

• impact;

• owner;

• dependencies;

• compatibility consideration;

• migration consideration;

• approval requirement;

• version identity;

• traceability.

Canonical distinction:

Evolution Driver

↓

Why change is considered

Change Architecture

↓

What change is being proposed

-

How its impact and boundaries are represented

H4 tidak menentukan apakah change akan disetujui.

⸻

4\. Source-Supported Foundation

Baseline secara eksplisit menetapkan untuk major change:

Change Reason

↓

Impact Analysis

↓

Owner

↓

Approval

↓

Versioned Change

Minimum requirements:

• reason for change;

• impact analysis;

• owner;

• approval;

• versioning.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa backward compatibility harus dipertahankan sejauh memungkinkan, dan jika compatibility tidak dapat dipertahankan maka migration mechanism harus tersedia dan traceable.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

↓

H7

Change Control

Implementation

H4 tidak memiliki primary ownership terhadap:

• authorization;

8\. Current State

Setiap material change harus memiliki pemahaman terhadap state yang sedang berlaku.

Minimum representation:

Current State

↓

Existing Governance Condition

Current state dapat mencakup:

• applicable policy;

• applicable standard;

• existing control;

• existing certification mechanism;

• existing compliance mechanism;

• existing authority;

• existing ownership;

• existing dependency;

• existing governance relationship.

Current state menjadi baseline untuk impact analysis.

⸻

9\. Target State

Setiap material change harus mendefinisikan intended target state.

Current State

↓

Proposed Change

↓

Target State

Target state harus cukup jelas untuk menjawab:

• apa yang berubah;

• apa yang tetap;

• governance mechanism apa yang ditambahkan;

• governance mechanism apa yang diubah;

• governance mechanism apa yang retired;

• relationship apa yang berubah.

Target state bukan implementation plan.

⸻

10\. Change Objective

Setiap change harus memiliki objective yang eksplisit.

Canonical pattern:

Driver

↓

Problem / Opportunity

↓

Change Objective

↓

Target State

Objective harus menjelaskan outcome yang ingin dicapai.

Tidak diperbolehkan menggunakan:

• vague objective;

• undocumented preference;

• convenience-only rationale.

⸻

11\. Change Reason

Reason for change merupakan mandatory foundation untuk material change.

Reason harus menjelaskan:

• driver;

• context;

• governance concern;

• why existing state is insufficient;

• expected purpose of change.

Baseline secara eksplisit menetapkan reason for change sebagai minimum requirement major change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

12\. Change Scope

Change scope harus menentukan:

Included

Apa yang berubah.

Excluded

Apa yang tidak berubah.

Affected

Domain atau governance relationship yang terdampak.

Model:

Change

├── In Scope

├── Out of Scope

└── Affected

Scope harus mencegah change creep.

⸻

13\. Change Boundary

Change boundary harus menjaga:

• PD-03 governance boundary;

• Architecture Authority;

• operational ownership;

• Platform autonomy;

• organizational ownership.

Canonical rule:

Governance Change

≠

Automatic Domain Ownership Change

Governance requirement dapat memiliki impact terhadap domain lain tanpa mengambil alih ownership domain tersebut.

⸻

14\. Change Classification

Materiality perlu dipertimbangkan ketika membentuk change.

Conceptual classification:

Class Description

Minor Perubahan terbatas dengan low material impact

Standard Perubahan governance yang berada dalam established mechanism

Material Perubahan yang memiliki significant governance consequence

Major Perubahan yang membutuhkan formal controlled-change treatment

Structural Perubahan terhadap fundamental governance structure

Source qualification: exact classification taxonomy di atas tidak ditemukan sebagai taxonomy literal dalam baseline.

Classification ini merupakan bounded reconstruction.

Formal threshold harus ditetapkan oleh authority yang berlaku, bukan oleh H4 secara sepihak.

⸻

15\. Major Change

Major governance evolution wajib diperlakukan sebagai controlled change.

Minimum source-supported requirements:

Reason

-

Impact Analysis

-

Owner

-

Approval

-

Versioning

Performance Architecture Review.txt

H4 merepresentasikan requirement tersebut.

H5 menentukan applicable decision and approval governance.

⸻

16\. Change Object

Material change harus memiliki identifiable change object.

Conceptual model:

Change Object

├── Change ID

├── Reason

├── Driver

├── Current State

├── Target State

├── Scope

├── Impact

├── Dependencies

├── Owner

├── Authority Requirement

├── Approval Requirement

├── Compatibility

├── Migration

├── Version

├── Status

└── Traceability

Schema ini merupakan bounded reconstruction.

⸻

17\. Change Identity

Setiap material change harus memiliki identity yang stabil.

Minimum conceptual identity:

Change ID

Version

Status

Change identity diperlukan agar:

• review dapat merujuk change yang tepat;

• implementation dapat ditelusuri;

• version history dapat dipertahankan;

• superseded change dapat dibedakan dari active change.

⸻

18\. Change Versioning

Versioning merupakan explicit source-supported requirement untuk major change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Versioning harus membedakan:

Change Proposal

↓

Reviewed Change

↓

Approved Change

↓

Implemented Change

↓

Validated Change

Versioning tidak berarti setiap minor edit harus menjadi major version.

Exact versioning convention berada pada applicable documentation/change-control mechanism.

⸻

19\. Change State

Change state harus dapat dibedakan dari lifecycle state.

Conceptual change states:

Draft

↓

Defined

↓

Assessed

↓

Reviewable

↓

Approved

↓

Controlled

↓

Implemented

↓

Validated

↓

Closed

Status taxonomy di atas adalah bounded reconstruction.

H4 tidak memberikan status Approved tanpa H5 decision.

⸻

20\. Change Dependency

Change harus mengidentifikasi dependency yang relevan.

Dependency dapat mencakup:

• governance dependency;

• architecture dependency;

• lifecycle dependency;

• performance dependency;

• platform dependency;

• compliance dependency;

• organizational dependency.

Model:

Change

│

├── Dependency A

├── Dependency B

└── Dependency C

Dependency tidak sama dengan ownership.

21\. Cross-Platform Change

Jika governance evolution memengaruhi Platform lain:

PD-03 Change

↓

Cross-Platform Impact

↓

Applicable Platform Owners

↓

Coordination

↓

Applicable Authority

PD-03 tidak boleh menggunakan governance evolution untuk mengambil alih execution domain Platform lain.

Cross-platform impact harus ditangani melalui applicable coordination mechanism.

⸻

22\. Impact Architecture

Impact Analysis merupakan explicit source-supported requirement untuk major change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

H4 harus menyediakan struktur untuk impact analysis.

Minimum dimensions:

Governance Impact

Architecture Impact

Organization Impact

Lifecycle Impact

Performance Impact

Compliance Impact

Integration Impact

Operational Impact

Platform Impact

Tidak semua dimension wajib memiliki material impact.

Yang penting adalah impact yang relevan dapat terlihat.

23\. Governance Impact

Assess:

• policy impact;

• standard impact;

• control impact;

• certification impact;

• compliance impact;

• approval impact;

• accountability impact;

• authority impact.

Model:

Change

↓

Governance Impact

├── Policy

├── Standards

├── Control

├── Certification

└── Compliance

Contoh:

Governance Change

↓

Architecture Impact

↓

Applicable Architecture Governance

H4 tidak menggantikan Architecture Authority.

25\. Organization Impact

Assess potential impact terhadap:

• responsibility;

• accountability;

• role;

• reporting;

• ownership;

• organizational structure.

Governance change tidak otomatis mengubah organization architecture.

Jika organization change diperlukan, gunakan applicable organizational mechanism.

26\. Lifecycle Impact

Change harus mempertimbangkan apakah governance evolution mempengaruhi lifecycle condition.

Contoh:

Governance Change

↓

Lifecycle Implication

↓

Lifecycle Assessment

H4 tidak mengambil alih Lifecycle Authority.

Change dapat memiliki performance implication.

Contoh:

• review overhead;

• compliance workload;

• governance efficiency;

• decision latency.

Performance impact menjadi input bagi applicable Performance Architecture.

H4 tidak menetapkan performance target.

• requirement alignment;

• evidence requirement;

• control obligation;

Compliance impact harus dapat ditelusuri kepada applicable requirement.

29\. Integration Impact

Governance evolution dapat memengaruhi:

• platform interface;

• shared governance service;

• dependency;

• contract;

• certification integration;

Change

↓

Integration Impact

↓

Affected Platform

↓

Coordination

30\. Operational Impact

Governance change dapat menghasilkan:

• new process;

• additional control;

• new review;

• new evidence requirement;

• new approval step;

• changed operating burden.

Operational owner tetap bertanggung jawab atas execution.

Compatibility dapat dipertimbangkan terhadap:

33\. Migration Architecture

Jika compatibility tidak dapat dipertahankan, migration mechanism harus tersedia dan dapat ditelusuri.

Source mendukung requirement migration tersebut.

Migration architecture dapat mencakup:

Current State

↓

Migration Preparation

↓

Transition

↓

Target State

↓

Validation

Migration tidak boleh dianggap complete hanya karena target state telah ditulis.

34\. Migration Boundary

H4 mendefinisikan migration requirement secara architectural.

H4 tidak menjalankan migration.

Migration Architecture
