35\. Rollback / Reversal Consideration

Material change harus mempertimbangkan consequence jika target state tidak dapat dipertahankan.

Conceptual model:

Approved Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Failure / Regression

↓

Mitigation / Reversal / Migration

Exact rollback mechanism bukan H4 authority.

Jika rollback tidak feasible, alternative mitigation atau migration harus dapat diidentifikasi.

⸻

36\. Non-Regression

Change architecture harus mengidentifikasi potential regression.

Minimum concern:

• governance integrity;

• authority clarity;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• compliance;

• traceability;

• Platform autonomy;

• canonical consistency.

Jika regression tidak dapat dihindari, rationale, impact assessment, authority decision, migration or mitigation, dan consequence harus terdokumentasi.

⸻

37\. Ownership Architecture

Owner merupakan explicit source-supported requirement untuk major change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Change architecture harus dapat menjawab:

Who owns the change?

Ownership harus dibedakan:

Ownership Meaning

Change Owner Memiliki change matter

Governance Owner Memiliki governance concern

Domain Owner Memiliki affected domain

Implementation Owner Menjalankan implementation

Reviewer Melakukan independent review

Authority Memiliki decision authority

H4 tidak mengubah ownership hanya karena change memiliki cross-platform impact.

⸻

38\. Authority Requirement

Change architecture harus merepresentasikan authority yang dibutuhkan.

Canonical distinction:

Authority Requirement

≠

Authority Decision

H4 menjawab:

authority apa yang dibutuhkan?

H5 menjawab:

apakah authority tersebut memberikan decision?

H4 tidak menciptakan authority baru.

⸻

39\. Approval Requirement

Approval requirement merupakan source-supported requirement untuk major change.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

H4 harus dapat menunjukkan:

Change

↓

Required Approval

↓

Applicable Authority

Namun:

Approval Required

≠

Approval Granted

Approval granted hanya dapat berasal dari applicable decision mechanism.

⸻

40\. Reviewability

Change architecture harus membuat change cukup jelas untuk review.

Reviewer harus dapat memahami:

• reason;

• driver;

• scope;

• target state;

• impact;

• owner;

• dependencies;

• compatibility;

• migration;

• required authority.

Change yang tidak cukup defined tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai review-ready material change.

• documented requirement;

49\. Change State Integrity

Change state harus konsisten dengan decision state.

Tidak diperbolehkan:

Change Status = Approved

Decision Record = Missing

atau:

Change Status = Implemented

Approval = Missing

untuk material controlled change.

State inconsistency harus menjadi governance finding.

⸻

50\. Change Conflict

Jika change candidate bertentangan dengan:

• canonical architecture;

• governance policy;

• standards;

• executive decision;

• existing authority;

• existing ownership;

maka conflict harus diidentifikasi sebelum approval.

Canonical-first principle berlaku.

Supporting documentation tidak boleh secara informal mengalahkan canonical authority.

⸻

51\. Change Duplication

Sebelum membuat change baru:

Search Existing Change

↓

Search Existing Decision

↓

Search Existing Governance Mechanism

↓

Determine Reuse / Adaptation

↓

Create New Change if Justified

Ini mengikuti prinsip Reference Before Duplication.

52\. Change Supersession

Jika change baru menggantikan change lama:

Change A

↓

Superseded

↓

Change B

↓

Current

Historical record Change A tetap harus dapat ditelusuri.

Supersession bukan deletion.

⸻

53\. Change Retirement

Change object dapat retired apabila:

• superseded;

• cancelled;

• rejected;

• obsolete;

• requirement withdrawn.

Retirement harus mempertahankan historical traceability.

⸻

54\. Change Cancellation

Change dapat dibatalkan apabila:

• driver no longer valid;

• requirement withdrawn;

• impact unacceptable;

• duplicate;

• better alternative exists;

• authority rejects proposal.

Cancellation harus memiliki reason.

⸻

55\. Change Rejection

Rejection merupakan decision outcome, bukan H4 decision.

H4 hanya menyediakan structure agar rejection dapat ditelusuri terhadap:

• reason;

• evidence;

• impact;

• decision.

⸻

56\. Change Alternatives

Material change sebaiknya dapat dibandingkan dengan reasonable alternatives.

Conceptual structure:

Option A

Option B

Option C

↓

Impact Comparison

↓

Preferred Change Candidate

Alternative analysis membantu menghindari premature commitment.

Exact option-selection mechanism berada pada H5.

Change architecture harus mengidentifikasi apakah change menghasilkan local benefit tetapi menimbulkan system-level consequence.

Local Benefit

-

Cross-Domain Impact

H4 tidak boleh mendefinisikan change hanya berdasarkan local convenience.

Change architecture harus mempertimbangkan apakah existing governance capability cukup stabil sebelum expansion.

Stability Before Expansion.

Change architecture harus terlebih dahulu mencari existing canonical mechanism.

↓

Reuse?

├── YES → Adapt / Reuse

└── NO → Justify New Change

Source mendukung prinsip Reference Before Duplication.

60\. Change Quality Gate

Sebelum masuk H5, material change minimal harus memiliki:

Requirement Status

Legitimate driver Required

Reason Required

Scope Required

Current state Required

Target state Required

Impact structure Required

Owner Required

Dependencies Required

Compatibility Required

Migration if needed Required

Authority requirement Required

Approval requirement Required

Version identity Required

Evidence Required

Jika requirement material belum tersedia:

Change

↓

NOT REVIEW-READY

bukan:

Change

↓

AUTO-APPROVED

⸻

61\. Change Architecture Quality Criteria

H4 dianggap structurally sound apabila:

1\. change memiliki legitimate reason;

2\. driver dapat ditelusuri;

3\. current state jelas;

4\. target state jelas;

5\. scope bounded;

6\. impact dapat dianalisis;

7\. owner jelas;

8\. dependencies terlihat;

9\. compatibility dipertimbangkan;

10\. migration disiapkan jika diperlukan;

11\. authority requirement terlihat;

12\. approval requirement terlihat;

13\. versioning tersedia;

14\. traceability tersedia;

15\. Platform autonomy terjaga.

⸻

62\. Anti-Patterns

H4 melarang:

62.1 Change Without Driver

Preference

↓

Change

Tidak legitimate.

62.2 Change Without Impact

Material change tidak boleh masuk approval tanpa impact consideration.

62.3 Change Without Owner

Material change harus memiliki owner.

62.4 Change Without Approval Path

Major change harus memiliki applicable approval requirement.

62.5 Approval as Architecture

Change definition bukan approval.

62.6 Documentation as Authority

Dokumentasi tidak otomatis menjadi authority.

62.7 Implementation as Authorization

Implementasi tidak membuktikan authorization.

62.8 Dependency as Ownership

Cross-platform impact tidak mentransfer ownership.

62.9 Compatibility Ignored

Breaking change tanpa migration consideration tidak memenuhi controlled evolution principle.

62.10 Silent Drift

Material change tidak boleh terjadi tanpa traceable change record.

74\. Canonical Freeze Statement

H4 — Evolution Change Architecture defines the bounded structure through which a qualified Evolution Driver is translated into a reviewable and traceable governance change candidate, including reason, scope, current state, target state, impact, ownership, dependencies, compatibility, migration, authority requirement, approval requirement, and version identity.

H4 does not authorize change, execute change, or determine change success. It prepares the change architecture for applicable decision governance, control, implementation, and validation.

H4 is FROZEN as a Bounded Domain Reconstruction with Source Qualification.

⸻

75\. Construction Boundary

H4 must not be used to infer:

• automatic approval;

• new authority;

• ownership transfer;

• implementation authority;

• mandatory technical architecture;

• operational command;

• final compliance acceptance;

• final success.

Those concerns remain under the applicable authority and downstream Part H sections.

\# 1. Purpose

H5 menetapkan governance architecture untuk menentukan

apakah suatu Evolution Change Candidate:

\- dapat diterima;

\- harus direvisi;

\- harus ditolak;

\- harus dieskalasikan;

\- atau dapat diteruskan menuju controlled implementation.

H5 menjawab:

\> \*\*Siapa yang berwenang memutuskan evolution, berdasarkan

\> evidence, impact, ownership, authority, dan applicable review?\*\*

H5 berada di antara:

\`\`\`text

H4 — Evolution Change Architecture

↓

H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

↓

H6 — Evolution Review

↓

H7 — Evolution Control

H5 tidak mengimplementasikan change.

H5 tidak menggantikan Architecture Authority.

H5 tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

3\. Definition

Evolution Decision Governance adalah struktur governance yang menentukan bagaimana Evolution Change Candidate diperiksa terhadap:

• legitimate driver;

• scope;

• impact;

• ownership;

• authority;

• applicable review;

• compatibility;

• governance integrity;

• Platform autonomy;

• applicable approval requirement.

Output utama H5 adalah:

Evolution Decision

Bukan:

Evolution Implementation

4\. Source-Supported Decision Foundation

Baseline menetapkan bahwa governance evolution harus:

0\. memiliki legitimate reason;

0\. memiliki identifiable driver;

0\. dapat dianalisis dampaknya;

0\. memiliki owner;

0\. memiliki authority yang sesuai;

0\. melalui review yang applicable;

0\. menghasilkan traceable decision;

0\. mempertahankan governance integrity;

0\. mempertahankan Platform autonomy;

0\. menghasilkan evidence.

11\. Decision Context

Decision harus menjelaskan context.

• current governance state;

• applicable constraints.

Context harus cukup untuk memungkinkan authority membuat decision yang informed.

Decision harus menggunakan evidence atau rationale yang dapat ditelusuri.

Source menetapkan Evidence-Based Decision sebagai decision principle.

• governance findings;

• compliance findings;

• review findings;

• lifecycle evidence;

• integration findings;

• audit evidence;

• documented requirement;

• risk evidence.

33\. Canonical Authority Precedence

Jika decision conflict dengan canonical authority:

Higher Authority

↓

Lower Authority

Lower-level document tidak boleh secara informal mengalahkan higher-level authority.

Constitutional Layer

↓

Canonical Layer

↓

Governance Layer

↓

Engineering Layer

↓

Strategic Layer

↓

Implementation Layer

Lower layers inherit higher-level constraints dan tidak boleh melemahkan atau override higher-level rules tanpa explicit authorization.

54\. Decision Integrity Gate

Decision hanya valid apabila:

55\. Decision Anti-Patterns

55.1 Self-Authorization

Change Owner

↓

Self Approval

Tidak diperbolehkan jika authority tidak mengizinkan.

55.2 Authority After Decision

Tidak boleh mencari authority setelah decision dibuat.

55.3 Evidence-Free Decision

Material evolution tidak boleh diputuskan tanpa evidence atau rationale yang dapat dipertanggungjawabkan.

55.4 Silent Approval

Approval harus memiliki record.

55.5 Implicit Ownership Transfer

Governance decision tidak otomatis memindahkan ownership.

55.6 Canonical Override

Supporting document tidak boleh override canonical authority.

55.7 Implementation as Approval

Implementasi bukan bukti bahwa approval telah diberikan.

55.8 Decision Without Closure

Decision lifecycle harus dapat ditutup atau direassess.

• authority tidak cukup;

64\. Relationship to H4

H4 answers:

What is the proposed change?

H5 answers:

Should the change be authorized, by whom, and under what governance conditions?

Therefore:

H4 = Change Architecture

H5 = Decision Governance

H5 harus menggunakan H4 sebagai source of change definition.

⸻

65\. Relationship to H6

H6 answers:

Is the decision/change still valid under applicable review?

H5 does not replace H6.

⸻

66\. Relationship to H7

H7 answers:

How is an approved change controlled?

H5 only authorizes the transition into controlled change.

⸻

67\. Relationship to H8

H8 answers:

How is the approved and controlled change implemented?

H5 has no implementation authority.

⸻

68\. Relationship to H9

H9 manages improvement and expansion arising from evidence and evolution outcomes.

H5 supplies decision history as one of its inputs.

⸻

69\. Relationship to H10

H10 determines whether the evolution achieved its intended success criteria.

H5 defines decision outcome, not success outcome.

74\. Canonical Decision Statement

PD-03 Evolution Decision Governance ensures that governance evolution decisions are made by applicable authority, based on legitimate drivers, evidence, impact analysis, explicit ownership, applicable review, and traceable approval, while preserving canonical authority, Architecture Authority, domain ownership, operational ownership, and Platform autonomy.

\# 1. Purpose

H5 menetapkan governance architecture untuk menentukan

apakah suatu Evolution Change Candidate:

\- dapat diterima;

\- harus direvisi;

\- harus ditolak;

\- harus dieskalasikan;

\- atau dapat diteruskan menuju controlled implementation.

H5 menjawab:

\> \*\*Siapa yang berwenang memutuskan evolution, berdasarkan

\> evidence, impact, ownership, authority, dan applicable review?\*\*

H5 berada di antara:

\`\`\`text

H4 — Evolution Change Architecture

↓

H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

↓

H6 — Evolution Review

↓

H7 — Evolution Control

H5 tidak mengimplementasikan change.

H5 tidak menggantikan Architecture Authority.

H5 tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

3\. Definition

Evolution Decision Governance adalah struktur governance yang menentukan bagaimana Evolution Change Candidate diperiksa terhadap:

• legitimate driver;

• scope;

• impact;

• ownership;

• authority;

• applicable review;

• compatibility;

• governance integrity;

• Platform autonomy;

• applicable approval requirement.

Output utama H5 adalah:

Evolution Decision

Bukan:

Evolution Implementation

⸻

4\. Source-Supported Decision Foundation

Baseline menetapkan bahwa governance evolution harus:

1\. memiliki legitimate reason;

2\. memiliki identifiable driver;

3\. dapat dianalisis dampaknya;

4\. memiliki owner;

5\. memiliki authority yang sesuai;

6\. melalui review yang applicable;

7\. menghasilkan traceable decision;

8\. mempertahankan governance integrity;

9\. mempertahankan Platform autonomy;

10\. menghasilkan evidence.

Untuk major change, minimum requirement mencakup:

Reason

-

Impact Analysis

-

Owner

-

Approval

-

Versioning

sebagai approved evolution.

• decision status.

≠

≠

Setiap material evolution decision harus memiliki identifiable decision object.

Decision Rationale

Impact Assessment

Conditions

Publication Status

9\. Decision Request

Evolution decision dimulai melalui:

Evolution Decision Request

Request harus mereferensikan H4 change object.

Minimal:

• Change ID;

• reason;

• driver;

• scope;

• impact;

• owner;

• requested authority;

• required review;

• evidence.

⸻

10\. Decision Validation

Sebelum substantive review:

Decision Request

↓

Validation

Validation memastikan:

• change object valid;

• owner tersedia;

• evidence tersedia;

• scope bounded;

• impact tersedia;

• authority requirement identifiable.

Jika tidak valid:

NOT DECISION-READY

bukan:

APPROVED

⸻

11\. Decision Context

Decision harus menjelaskan context.

Context mencakup:

• current governance state;

• evolution driver;

• reason;

• affected domain;

• strategic context;

• governance context;

• relevant dependencies;

• applicable constraints.

Context harus cukup untuk memungkinkan authority membuat decision yang informed.

⸻

12\. Evidence-Based Decision

Decision harus menggunakan evidence atau rationale yang dapat ditelusuri.

Source menetapkan Evidence-Based Decision sebagai decision principle.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• governance findings;

• compliance findings;

• review findings;

• performance evidence;

• lifecycle evidence;

• integration findings;

• audit evidence;

• documented requirement;

• risk evidence.

H5 tidak mengarang evidence.

⸻

13\. Impact Assessment

Decision tidak boleh dipisahkan dari impact.

Change

↓

Impact Analysis

↓

Decision

Impact dapat mencakup:

• governance;

• architecture;

• compliance;

• lifecycle;

• performance;

• integration;

• organization;

• operational;

• cross-platform.

H5 menggunakan hasil H4.

H5 tidak menggantikan detailed impact architecture H4.

⸻

14\. Authority Identification

Setiap evolution decision harus menentukan:

Authority siapa yang berwenang memutuskan?

Authority hierarchy harus mengikuti applicable Governance Architecture.

Authority tidak dapat digunakan di luar Domain Boundary.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Canonical pattern:

Decision Request

↓

Determine Domain

↓

Determine Authority

↓

Authority Review

↓

Decision

15\. Authority Before Decision

Tidak boleh:

Decision

↓

Find Authority Afterwards

Yang benar:

Authority

↓

Decision Scope

↓

Decision

H5 harus memastikan decision maker legitimate sebelum decision diberikan.

⸻

16\. Decision Ownership

Decision Owner harus berbeda secara konseptual dari Approving Authority.

Role Primary Responsibility

Change Owner Memiliki change matter

Decision Owner Memiliki decision matter

Reviewer Menilai evidence dan compliance

Approving Authority Memberikan approval

Custodian Menjaga record

Implementation Owner Menjalankan approved change

Ownership structure harus eksplisit.

⸻

Approval adalah authorization terhadap change dalam scope authority yang berlaku.

Approval Required

≠

Approval Granted

Major change memerlukan approval sebagai minimum requirement.

19\. Decision Options

Decision harus memiliki outcome yang eksplisit.

Minimum conceptual outcomes:

APPROVE

REJECT

RETURN_FOR_REVISION

ESCALATE

DEFER

Taxonomy ini merupakan bounded reconstruction.

Applicable authority dapat menetapkan terminology yang lebih spesifik.

20\. Approve

Jika approved:

Decision

↓

Approved

↓

Controlled Change

Approval harus memiliki:

• authority;

• date;

• decision record;

• scope;

• conditions;

• version.

⸻

21\. Reject

Jika rejected:

Decision

↓

Rejected

Rejection harus memiliki rationale.

Rejected change tidak boleh diteruskan ke implementation sebagai approved change.

⸻

22\. Return for Revision

Jika evidence atau change architecture belum memadai:

Decision Review

↓

Return for Revision

↓

H4

23\. Escalate

Jika authority tidak memadai atau conflict unresolved:

Local Authority

↓

Escalation

↓

Applicable Higher Authority

Escalation didasarkan pada authority dan impact, bukan sekadar organizational preference.

⸻

24\. Defer

Decision dapat ditunda apabila:

• evidence belum cukup;

• dependency belum siap;

• capability belum stabil;

• related decision belum selesai;

• timing belum appropriate.

Deferred ≠ rejected.

-

≠

• compatibility;

• applicable compliance constraint.

Evolution Decision

↓

Architecture Impact

Architecture Authority

Governance Authority

≠

31\. Domain Ownership Preservation

Decision tidak boleh mengubah ownership secara implisit.

Governance Decision

↓

Governance Requirement

↓

Domain Owner

↓

Execution

Governance responsibility tidak otomatis mentransfer domain ownership.

32\. Platform Autonomy

Evolution decision harus mempertahankan Platform autonomy.

Jika decision membutuhkan cross-platform action:

• affected owner harus identifiable;

• authority harus applicable;

• dependency harus explicit;

• execution tetap dilakukan oleh domain owner.

⸻

33\. Canonical Authority Precedence

Jika decision conflict dengan canonical authority:

Higher Authority

↓

Lower Authority

Lower-level document tidak boleh secara informal mengalahkan higher-level authority.

Source menetapkan hierarchy:

Constitutional Layer

↓

Canonical Layer

↓

Governance Layer

↓

Engineering Layer

↓

Strategic Layer

↓

Implementation Layer

Lower layers inherit higher-level constraints dan tidak boleh melemahkan atau override higher-level rules tanpa explicit authorization.

Identify Conflict

Classify Authority

Resolve or Escalate

↓

Record Outcome

Conflict tidak boleh diselesaikan melalui undocumented override.

sebagai legitimate option apabila existing governance state masih memadai.

• why;

• by which authority;

• with what impact;

• under which conditions.

• discoverability;

• traceability;

• authority visibility;

• downstream awareness.

Publication tidak mengubah decision menjadi lebih authoritative daripada authority yang mengeluarkannya.

• melebihi delegated scope;

• menghapus accountability;

• override higher authority;

• mengubah constitutional boundary.

53\. Decision Quality Gate

Sebelum approval, minimum criteria:

Criterion Required

Legitimate driver YES

Evidence / rationale YES

Change object YES

Scope YES

Impact YES

Owner YES

Authority identified YES

Applicable review YES

Compatibility consideration YES

Migration consideration if required YES

Decision rationale YES

Approval requirement YES

Traceability YES

Version YES for material change

Jika criteria belum terpenuhi:

Decision

↓

NOT READY

⸻

54\. Decision Integrity Gate

Decision hanya valid apabila:

1\. authority legitimate;

2\. decision berada dalam authority scope;

3\. evidence tersedia;

4\. impact dipertimbangkan;

5\. ownership jelas;

6\. applicable review selesai;

7\. canonical precedence dipatuhi;

8\. Platform autonomy terjaga;

9\. outcome terdokumentasi;

10\. decision dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

55\. Decision Anti-Patterns

55.1 Self-Authorization

Change Owner

↓

Self Approval

Tidak diperbolehkan jika authority tidak mengizinkan.

55.2 Authority After Decision

Tidak boleh mencari authority setelah decision dibuat.

55.3 Evidence-Free Decision

Material evolution tidak boleh diputuskan tanpa evidence atau rationale yang dapat dipertanggungjawabkan.

55.4 Silent Approval

Approval harus memiliki record.

55.5 Implicit Ownership Transfer

Governance decision tidak otomatis memindahkan ownership.

55.6 Canonical Override

Supporting document tidak boleh override canonical authority.

55.7 Implementation as Approval

Implementasi bukan bukti bahwa approval telah diberikan.

55.8 Decision Without Closure

Decision lifecycle harus dapat ditutup atau direassess.

⸻

56\. Decision Escalation

Escalation digunakan apabila:

• authority tidak cukup;

• conflict unresolved;

• cross-platform impact material;

• constitutional conflict;

• significant governance risk;

• unresolved ownership conflict.

Canonical model:

Local Review

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Escalation

↓

Higher Authority

↓

Decision

Escalation tidak boleh menjadi shortcut untuk bypass normal review.

⸻

Decision dianggap closed apabila:

• outcome tercatat;

• approval record tersedia;

• conditions ditangani;

• downstream action assigned;

• implementation handoff selesai;

• validation responsibility assigned.

H5 tidak menentukan final success.

64\. Relationship to H4

H4 answers:

What is the proposed change?

H5 answers:

Should the change be authorized, by whom, and under what governance conditions?

Therefore:

H4 = Change Architecture

H5 = Decision Governance

H5 harus menggunakan H4 sebagai source of change definition.

⸻

65\. Relationship to H6

H6 answers:

Is the decision/change still valid under applicable review?

H5 does not replace H6.

⸻

66\. Relationship to H7

H7 answers:

How is an approved change controlled?

H5 only authorizes the transition into controlled change.

⸻

67\. Relationship to H8

H8 answers:

How is the approved and controlled change implemented?

H5 has no implementation authority.

⸻

68\. Relationship to H9

H9 manages improvement and expansion arising from evidence and evolution outcomes.

H5 supplies decision history as one of its inputs.

⸻

69\. Relationship to H10

H10 determines whether the evolution achieved its intended success criteria.

H5 defines decision outcome, not success outcome.

PD-03 Evolution Decision Governance ensures that governance evolution decisions are made by applicable authority, based on legitimate drivers, evidence, impact analysis, explicit ownership, applicable review, and traceable approval, while preserving canonical authority, Architecture Authority, domain ownership, operational ownership, and Platform autonomy.

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# Part H — Platform Evolution

\# H6 — Evolution Review

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* H6

\> \*\*Section Name:\*\* Evolution Review

\> \*\*Platform ID:\*\* PD-03

\> \*\*Official Name:\*\* Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Volume:\*\* Volume 3

\> \*\*Part:\*\* Part H — Platform Evolution

\> \*\*Predecessor:\*\* H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

\> \*\*Constitutional Parent:\*\* H1 — Evolution Constitution

\> \*\*Source-Fidelity Mode:\*\* Bounded Domain Reconstruction

\> \*\*Gold Standard Review:\*\* PASS WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED

\---

\# 1. Purpose

H6 menetapkan governance review mechanism untuk memastikan

bahwa Evolution Change dan Evolution Decision:

\- tetap valid;

\- tetap berada dalam authority boundary;

\- tetap sesuai dengan applicable governance requirement;

\- memiliki evidence yang memadai;

\- memiliki impact yang dipahami;

\- menjaga ownership;

\- menjaga Platform autonomy;

\- tetap traceable;

\- dan menghasilkan finding yang dapat ditindaklanjuti.

H6 menjawab:

\> \*\*Apakah evolution yang sedang dipertimbangkan, diputuskan,

\> atau dikendalikan tetap valid, compliant, dan consistent

\> terhadap governance architecture yang berlaku?\*\*

H6 bukan implementation mechanism.

H6 bukan decision authority.

H6 merupakan \*\*review and assurance mechanism\*\*.

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

Relationship:

\`\`\`text

H1 — Evolution Constitution

↓

H2 — Evolution Model

↓

H3 — Evolution Drivers

↓

H4 — Evolution Change Architecture

↓

H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

↓

H6 — Evolution Review

↓

H7 — Evolution Control

↓

H8 — Evolution Implementation

↓

H9 — Evolution Improvement & Expansion

↓

H10 — Evolution Success

H6 menerima input terutama dari H4 dan H5.

H6 menghasilkan:

• review finding;

• review conclusion;

• compliance assessment;

• improvement signal;

• escalation signal;

• reassessment requirement.

⸻

3\. Definition

Evolution Review adalah mekanisme governance untuk menilai apakah evolution tetap:

• justified;

• authorized;

• bounded;

• evidence-based;

• compliant;

• traceable;

• compatible;

• controlled.

Canonical distinction:

H5

Decision Governance

↓

H6

Review / Assurance

↓

H7

Change Control

H5 menentukan decision governance.

H6 memeriksa validity dan governance integrity.

H7 mengendalikan approved change.

⸻

4\. Source-Supported Foundation

Baseline/source mendukung evolution feedback loop:

Governance Operation

↓

Evidence

↓

Review

↓

Finding

↓

Evolution Assessment

↓

Controlled Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Validation

↓

New Governance State

↓

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa evolution harus menjaga:

• authority clarity;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• traceability;

• compliance;

• Platform autonomy;

• canonical consistency.

Jika regression tidak dapat dihindari, harus tersedia:

• explicit rationale;

• impact assessment;

• authority decision;

• migration or mitigation;

• documented consequence.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

5\. Review Objective

H6 memiliki objective:

ER-01 — Evolution Validity

Memastikan evolution masih memiliki legitimate basis.

ER-02 — Governance Compliance

Memastikan evolution tetap compliant terhadap applicable governance requirement.

ER-03 — Authority Integrity

Memastikan authority yang digunakan tetap legitimate.

ER-04 — Ownership Integrity

Memastikan ownership tidak berubah secara tidak sah.

ER-05 — Impact Integrity

Memastikan material impact tetap diketahui.

ER-06 — Traceability

Memastikan evolution dapat ditelusuri dari driver sampai review outcome.

ER-07 — Compatibility

Memastikan compatibility atau migration requirement tetap dipahami.

ER-08 — Controlled Evolution

Memastikan evolution tidak berubah menjadi uncontrolled drift.

⸻

6\. Review Boundary

H6 berada pada boundary:

Evolution Decision

↓

Evolution Review

↓

Evolution Control

H6 tidak mengubah approved decision secara sepihak.

Jika review menemukan material issue:

Finding

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Reassessment / Corrective Action / Escalation

⸻

7\. In Scope

H6 mencakup review terhadap:

• evolution rationale;

• evolution driver;

• change scope;

• current state;

• target state;

• impact assessment;

• ownership;

• authority;

• approval;

• evidence;

• compatibility;

• migration;

• governance compliance;

• canonical alignment;

• Platform autonomy;

• implementation readiness;

• review history;

• outstanding findings;

• corrective action;

• evolution outcome.

⸻

8\. Out of Scope

H6 tidak memiliki primary responsibility terhadap:

• technical implementation;

• operational execution;

• deployment;

• domain capability execution;

• implementation ownership;

• Architecture Authority decision;

• final performance ownership;

• final success declaration.

Boundary:

Review

≠

Execution

Review

≠

Implementation

Review

≠

Automatic Re-Authorization

Change ID

10\. Review Types

H6 dapat menggunakan review berdasarkan context.

10.1 Pre-Implementation Review

Memastikan approved evolution siap memasuki controlled implementation.

10.2 Change Review

Memeriksa apakah change tetap berada dalam approved scope.

10.3 Compliance Review

Memeriksa governance compliance.

10.4 Authority Review

Memeriksa authority integrity.

10.5 Impact Review

Memeriksa apakah material impact berubah.

10.6 Post-Decision Review

Memeriksa apakah decision tetap valid setelah evidence baru.

10.7 Post-Implementation Review

Memeriksa governance consequence setelah implementation.

10.8 Event-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh material event atau finding.

Taxonomy di atas merupakan bounded reconstruction dari source-supported review architecture.

⸻

11\. Review Trigger

Review dapat dipicu oleh:

• material change;

• new evidence;

• governance finding;

• compliance finding;

• authority conflict;

• ownership conflict;

• cross-platform impact;

• compatibility failure;

• migration issue;

• implementation deviation;

• major architecture evolution;

• lifecycle change;

• performance finding;

• recurring operational issue.

⸻

12\. Review Before Evolution

Material evolution harus melalui applicable review sebelum evolution dianggap fully controlled.

Canonical flow:

Evolution Need

↓

Change Architecture

↓

Decision

↓

Review

↓

Controlled Evolution

Review tidak boleh dihilangkan hanya karena evolution dianggap urgent, kecuali terdapat explicit authorized emergency mechanism.

⸻

13\. Evidence Requirement

Review harus berbasis evidence.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• governance records;

• decision records;

• compliance evidence;

• architecture evidence;

• lifecycle evidence;

• performance evidence;

• integration evidence;

• audit findings;

• implementation records;

• review history.

Source secara eksplisit menggunakan evidence sebagai foundation governance evolution.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

14\. Evidence Sufficiency

Reviewer harus menentukan apakah evidence cukup untuk mendukung conclusion.

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Review Incomplete

↓

Additional Evidence

Tidak diperbolehkan:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Manufactured Conclusion

⸻

15\. Review Criteria

Review criteria harus berasal dari applicable governance requirements.

Criteria dapat mencakup:

• authority;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• compliance;

• impact;

• traceability;

• compatibility;

• migration;

• canonical consistency;

• Platform autonomy;

• change control.

Exact criteria harus disesuaikan dengan jenis review.

⸻

16\. Review Scope

Setiap review harus mendefinisikan:

Included

Apa yang sedang direview.

Excluded

Apa yang tidak direview.

Affected

Domain yang dapat terdampak.

Model:

Review

├── Scope

├── Exclusions

└── Affected Domains

Review scope tidak boleh berkembang tanpa recorded scope change.

⸻

17\. Review Owner

Setiap review harus memiliki owner.

Review Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• review planning;

• evidence coordination;

• criteria application;

• finding management;

• conclusion;

• review record.

Review Owner tidak otomatis menjadi:

• decision authority;

• implementation owner;

• domain owner.

⸻

18\. Reviewer Independence

Untuk material review, independence harus dipertimbangkan.

Reviewer sebaiknya tidak menjadi satu-satunya pihak yang menilai pekerjaan yang ia sendiri implementasikan apabila independence diperlukan.

Model:

Implementation Owner

↓

X

Cannot automatically be sole independent reviewer

Independence requirement harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

19\. Authority Review

Review harus memeriksa:

• siapa authority yang digunakan;

• apakah authority valid;

• apakah decision berada dalam scope;

• apakah delegated authority masih valid;

• apakah higher authority constraint dipatuhi.

Authority boundary tidak boleh diperluas melalui review.

⸻

20\. Ownership Review

Reviewer harus memastikan:

Governance Owner

Domain Owner

Implementation Owner

Decision Authority

Reviewer

tidak menjadi ambiguous.

Governance responsibility tidak otomatis mentransfer domain ownership.

52\. Review Quality Controls

Setiap material Evolution Review harus diperiksa terhadap:

Q-01 — Evidence Sufficiency

Evidence cukup untuk conclusion.

Q-02 — Criteria Validity

Criteria applicable dan legitimate.

Q-03 — Scope Integrity

Review tidak keluar dari scope.

Q-04 — Authority Integrity

Reviewer dan decision authority berada dalam scope yang sah.

Q-05 — Ownership Clarity

Owner setiap finding jelas.

Q-06 — Traceability

Conclusion dapat ditelusuri.

Q-07 — Independence

Independence cukup terhadap materiality.

Q-08 — Materiality

Finding diperlakukan proporsional.

Q-09 — Follow-up

Finding memiliki action atau documented disposition.

53\. Review Failure States

Review harus berhenti atau ditunda apabila:

Evidence Insufficient

Criteria Invalid

Authority Unclear

Scope Ambiguous

Ownership Missing

Impact Unknown

Decision Record Missing

Compatibility Unknown

Dalam kondisi tersebut:

Do not manufacture a review conclusion.

⸻

54\. Review Anti-Patterns

54.1 Rubber-Stamp Review

Review tidak boleh hanya mengkonfirmasi decision tanpa memeriksa evidence.

54.2 Review Without Criteria

Review harus memiliki review criteria.

54.3 Review Without Evidence

Conclusion harus memiliki basis evidence.

54.4 Reviewer as Sole Executor

Independence harus dipertimbangkan untuk material review.

54.5 Review as Authority Expansion

Review tidak menciptakan authority baru.

54.6 Finding Without Owner

Material finding harus memiliki owner.

54.7 Finding Without Follow-up

Material finding tidak boleh hilang tanpa disposition.

54.8 Silent Closure

Closure harus traceable.

54.9 Review as Implementation

Reviewer tidak menjadi automatic implementation owner.

Governance Operation

↓

Evidence

↓

Review

72\. Canonical Review Statement

PD-03 Evolution Review provides evidence-based, traceable, and accountable review of governance evolution to verify authority, ownership, impact, compliance, compatibility, canonical consistency, and Platform autonomy, producing findings, conclusions, corrective actions, and reassessment signals without replacing decision authority or implementation ownership.

H6

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# Part H — Platform Evolution

\# H6 — Evolution Review

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* H6

\> \*\*Section Name:\*\* Evolution Review

\> \*\*Platform ID:\*\* PD-03

\> \*\*Official Name:\*\* Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Volume:\*\* Volume 3

\> \*\*Part:\*\* Part H — Platform Evolution

\> \*\*Predecessor:\*\* H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

\> \*\*Constitutional Parent:\*\* H1 — Evolution Constitution

\> \*\*Status:\*\* FROZEN

\> \*\*Classification:\*\* Evolution Review

\> \*\*Source-Fidelity Mode:\*\* Bounded Domain Reconstruction

\> \*\*Gold Standard Review:\*\* PASS WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED

\---

\# 1. Purpose

H6 menetapkan governance review mechanism untuk memastikan

bahwa Evolution Change dan Evolution Decision:

\- tetap valid;

\- tetap berada dalam authority boundary;

\- tetap sesuai dengan applicable governance requirement;

\- memiliki evidence yang memadai;

\- memiliki impact yang dipahami;

\- menjaga ownership;

\- menjaga Platform autonomy;

\- tetap traceable;

\- dan menghasilkan finding yang dapat ditindaklanjuti.

H6 menjawab:

\> \*\*Apakah evolution yang sedang dipertimbangkan, diputuskan,

\> atau dikendalikan tetap valid, compliant, dan consistent

\> terhadap governance architecture yang berlaku?\*\*

H6 bukan implementation mechanism.

H6 bukan decision authority.

H6 merupakan \*\*review and assurance mechanism\*\*.

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

Relationship:

\`\`\`text

H1 — Evolution Constitution

↓

H2 — Evolution Model

↓

H3 — Evolution Drivers

↓

H4 — Evolution Change Architecture

↓

H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

↓

H6 — Evolution Review

↓

H7 — Evolution Control

↓

H8 — Evolution Implementation

↓

H9 — Evolution Improvement & Expansion

↓

H10 — Evolution Success

H6 menerima input terutama dari H4 dan H5.

H6 menghasilkan:

• review finding;

• review conclusion;

• compliance assessment;

• improvement signal;

• escalation signal;

• reassessment requirement.

⸻

3\. Definition

Evolution Review adalah mekanisme governance untuk menilai apakah evolution tetap:

• justified;

• authorized;

• bounded;

• evidence-based;

• compliant;

• traceable;

• compatible;

• controlled.

Canonical distinction:

H5

Decision Governance

↓

H6

Review / Assurance

↓

H7

Change Control

H5 menentukan decision governance.

H6 memeriksa validity dan governance integrity.

H7 mengendalikan approved change.

⸻

4\. Source-Supported Foundation

Baseline/source mendukung evolution feedback loop:

Governance Operation

↓

Evidence

↓

Review

↓

Finding

↓

Evolution Assessment

↓

Controlled Change

↓

Implementation

↓

Validation

↓

New Governance State

↓

New Evidence

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa evolution harus menjaga:

• authority clarity;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• traceability;

• compliance;

• Platform autonomy;

• canonical consistency.

Jika regression tidak dapat dihindari, harus tersedia:

• explicit rationale;

• impact assessment;

• authority decision;

• migration or mitigation;

• documented consequence.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

5\. Review Objective

H6 memiliki objective:

ER-01 — Evolution Validity

Memastikan evolution masih memiliki legitimate basis.

ER-02 — Governance Compliance

Memastikan evolution tetap compliant terhadap applicable governance requirement.

ER-03 — Authority Integrity

Memastikan authority yang digunakan tetap legitimate.

ER-04 — Ownership Integrity

Memastikan ownership tidak berubah secara tidak sah.

ER-05 — Impact Integrity

Memastikan material impact tetap diketahui.

ER-06 — Traceability

Memastikan evolution dapat ditelusuri dari driver sampai review outcome.

ER-07 — Compatibility

Memastikan compatibility atau migration requirement tetap dipahami.

ER-08 — Controlled Evolution

Memastikan evolution tidak berubah menjadi uncontrolled drift.

⸻

6\. Review Boundary

H6 berada pada boundary:

Evolution Decision

↓

Evolution Review

↓

Evolution Control

H6 tidak mengubah approved decision secara sepihak.

Jika review menemukan material issue:

Finding

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Reassessment / Corrective Action / Escalation

⸻

7\. In Scope

H6 mencakup review terhadap:

• evolution rationale;

• evolution driver;

• change scope;

• current state;

• target state;

• impact assessment;

• ownership;

• authority;

• approval;

• evidence;

• compatibility;

• migration;

• governance compliance;

• canonical alignment;

• Platform autonomy;

• implementation readiness;

• review history;

• outstanding findings;

• corrective action;

• evolution outcome.

⸻

• technical implementation;

• operational execution;

• deployment;

• domain capability execution;

• implementation ownership;

• final success declaration.

Review

≠

Execution

Review

≠

Implementation

Review

≠

Change ID

Decision ID

Review Type

10\. Review Types

H6 dapat menggunakan review berdasarkan context.

10.1 Pre-Implementation Review

Memastikan approved evolution siap memasuki controlled implementation.

10.2 Change Review

Memeriksa apakah change tetap berada dalam approved scope.

10.3 Compliance Review

Memeriksa governance compliance.

10.4 Authority Review

Memeriksa authority integrity.

10.5 Impact Review

Memeriksa apakah material impact berubah.

10.6 Post-Decision Review

Memeriksa apakah decision tetap valid setelah evidence baru.

10.7 Post-Implementation Review

Memeriksa governance consequence setelah implementation.

10.8 Event-Based Review

Review yang dipicu oleh material event atau finding.

Taxonomy di atas merupakan bounded reconstruction dari source-supported review architecture.

⸻

11\. Review Trigger

Review dapat dipicu oleh:

• material change;

• new evidence;

• governance finding;

• compliance finding;

• authority conflict;

• ownership conflict;

• cross-platform impact;

• compatibility failure;

• migration issue;

• implementation deviation;

• major architecture evolution;

• lifecycle change;

• performance finding;

• recurring operational issue.

⸻

12\. Review Before Evolution

Material evolution harus melalui applicable review sebelum evolution dianggap fully controlled.

Canonical flow:

Evolution Need

↓

Change Architecture

↓

Decision

↓

Review

↓

Controlled Evolution

Review tidak boleh dihilangkan hanya karena evolution dianggap urgent, kecuali terdapat explicit authorized emergency mechanism.

⸻

13\. Evidence Requirement

Review harus berbasis evidence.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• governance records;

• decision records;

• compliance evidence;

• architecture evidence;

• lifecycle evidence;

• performance evidence;

• integration evidence;

• audit findings;

• implementation records;

• review history.

Source secara eksplisit menggunakan evidence sebagai foundation governance evolution.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

14\. Evidence Sufficiency

Reviewer harus menentukan apakah evidence cukup untuk mendukung conclusion.

Jika evidence tidak cukup:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Review Incomplete

↓

Additional Evidence

Tidak diperbolehkan:

Insufficient Evidence

↓

Manufactured Conclusion

⸻

15\. Review Criteria

Review criteria harus berasal dari applicable governance requirements.

Criteria dapat mencakup:

• authority;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• compliance;

• impact;

• traceability;

• compatibility;

• migration;

• canonical consistency;

• Platform autonomy;

• change control.

Exact criteria harus disesuaikan dengan jenis review.

⸻

16\. Review Scope

Setiap review harus mendefinisikan:

Included

Apa yang sedang direview.

Excluded

Apa yang tidak direview.

Affected

Domain yang dapat terdampak.

Model:

Review

├── Scope

├── Exclusions

└── Affected Domains

Review scope tidak boleh berkembang tanpa recorded scope change.

⸻

17\. Review Owner

Setiap review harus memiliki owner.

Review Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• review planning;

• evidence coordination;

• criteria application;

• finding management;

• conclusion;

• review record.

Review Owner tidak otomatis menjadi:

• decision authority;

• implementation owner;

• domain owner.

⸻

18\. Reviewer Independence

Untuk material review, independence harus dipertimbangkan.

Reviewer sebaiknya tidak menjadi satu-satunya pihak yang menilai pekerjaan yang ia sendiri implementasikan apabila independence diperlukan.

Model:

Implementation Owner

↓

X

Cannot automatically be sole independent reviewer

Independence requirement harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

⸻

19\. Authority Review

Review harus memeriksa:

• siapa authority yang digunakan;

• apakah authority valid;

• apakah decision berada dalam scope;

• apakah delegated authority masih valid;

• apakah higher authority constraint dipatuhi.

Authority boundary tidak boleh diperluas melalui review.

⸻

20\. Ownership Review

Reviewer harus memastikan:

Governance Owner

Domain Owner

Implementation Owner

Decision Authority

Reviewer

tidak menjadi ambiguous.

Governance responsibility tidak otomatis mentransfer domain ownership.

52\. Review Quality Controls

Setiap material Evolution Review harus diperiksa terhadap:

Q-01 — Evidence Sufficiency

Evidence cukup untuk conclusion.

Q-02 — Criteria Validity

Criteria applicable dan legitimate.

Q-03 — Scope Integrity

Review tidak keluar dari scope.

Q-04 — Authority Integrity

Reviewer dan decision authority berada dalam scope yang sah.

Q-05 — Ownership Clarity

Owner setiap finding jelas.

Q-06 — Traceability

Conclusion dapat ditelusuri.

Q-07 — Independence

Independence cukup terhadap materiality.

Q-08 — Materiality

Finding diperlakukan proporsional.

Q-09 — Follow-up

Finding memiliki action atau documented disposition.

⸻

53\. Review Failure States

Review harus berhenti atau ditunda apabila:

Evidence Insufficient

Criteria Invalid

Authority Unclear

Scope Ambiguous

Ownership Missing

Impact Unknown

Decision Record Missing

Compatibility Unknown

Dalam kondisi tersebut:

Do not manufacture a review conclusion.

⸻

54\. Review Anti-Patterns

54.1 Rubber-Stamp Review

Review tidak boleh hanya mengkonfirmasi decision tanpa memeriksa evidence.

54.2 Review Without Criteria

Review harus memiliki review criteria.

54.3 Review Without Evidence

Conclusion harus memiliki basis evidence.

54.4 Reviewer as Sole Executor

Independence harus dipertimbangkan untuk material review.

54.5 Review as Authority Expansion

Review tidak menciptakan authority baru.

54.6 Finding Without Owner

Material finding harus memiliki owner.

54.7 Finding Without Follow-up

Material finding tidak boleh hilang tanpa disposition.

54.8 Silent Closure

Closure harus traceable.

54.9 Review as Implementation

Reviewer tidak menjadi automatic implementation owner.

⸻

72\. Canonical Review Statement

PD-03 Evolution Review provides evidence-based, traceable, and accountable review of governance evolution to verify authority, ownership, impact, compliance, compatibility, canonical consistency, and Platform autonomy, producing findings, conclusions, corrective actions, and reassessment signals without replacing decision authority or implementation ownership.

H7

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\# Part H — Platform Evolution

\# H7 — Evolution Control

\> \*\*Section ID:\*\* H7

\> \*\*Section Name:\*\* Evolution Control

\> \*\*Platform ID:\*\* PD-03

\> \*\*Official Name:\*\* Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Volume:\*\* Volume 3

\> \*\*Part:\*\* Part H — Platform Evolution

\> \*\*Predecessor:\*\* H6 — Evolution Review

\> \*\*Successor:\*\* H8 — Evolution Implementation

\> \*\*Constitutional Parent:\*\* H1 — Evolution Constitution

\> \*\*Decision Predecessor:\*\* H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

\> \*\*Status:\*\* FROZEN

\> \*\*Classification:\*\* Evolution Control

\> \*\*Source-Fidelity Mode:\*\* Bounded Domain Reconstruction

\> \*\*Gold Standard Review:\*\* PASS WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION

\> \*\*Freeze Decision:\*\* APPROVED

\---

\# 1. Purpose

H7 menetapkan \*\*Evolution Control Architecture\*\* untuk

memastikan bahwa governance evolution yang telah memperoleh

applicable decision dan review dapat dikendalikan secara

terstruktur sebelum dan selama implementation.

H7 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana evolution yang telah diotorisasi dikendalikan

\> agar tetap berada dalam approved scope, authority boundary,

\> ownership boundary, impact boundary, dan governance

\> requirements sampai implementation dan validation selesai?\*\*

H7 bukan:

\- decision authority;

\- review authority;

\- implementation mechanism;

\- operational execution mechanism;

\- final success assessment.

H7 adalah \*\*control layer\*\*.

\---

\# 2. Constitutional Position

Relationship:

\`\`\`text

H1 — Evolution Constitution

↓

H4 — Evolution Change Architecture

↓

H5 — Evolution Decision Governance

↓

H6 — Evolution Review

↓

H7 — Evolution Control

↓

H8 — Evolution Implementation

↓

H9 — Evolution Improvement & Expansion

↓

H10 — Evolution Success

H7 menerima hasil:

• change definition dari H4;

• decision governance dari H5;

• review conclusion dari H6.

H7 menghasilkan:

• control state;

• control conditions;

• control gates;

• control evidence;

• deviation handling;

• implementation authorization signal;

• validation requirements.

⸻

3\. Definition

Evolution Control adalah mekanisme untuk menjaga agar evolution yang telah disetujui tetap:

• within approved scope;

• within authority;

• within ownership boundary;

• within impact boundary;

• traceable;

• compliant;

• compatible;

• controlled;

• reversible or mitigated where required.

Canonical distinction:

H5

Decision

↓

H6

Review

↓

H7

Control

↓

H8

Implementation

H7 tidak menciptakan approval.

H7 memastikan approval yang telah diberikan tidak berubah menjadi uncontrolled change.

⸻

4\. Source-Supported Foundation

Baseline menetapkan Controlled Change sebagai prinsip evolution.

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa major change minimal memerlukan:

• reason for change;

• impact analysis;

• owner;

• approval;

• versioning.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan:

• authority clarity;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• traceability;

• compliance;

• Platform autonomy;

• canonical consistency.

Jika regression tidak dapat dihindari, diperlukan:

• explicit rationale;

• impact assessment;

• authority decision;

• migration or mitigation;

• documented consequence.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Dengan demikian H7 memiliki foundation source yang kuat meskipun exact H7 heading tidak ditemukan secara literal.

⸻

5\. Control Objective

H7 memiliki objective berikut.

EC-01 — Scope Control

Memastikan implementation tidak keluar dari approved scope.

EC-02 — Authority Control

Memastikan change tetap berada dalam authority yang sah.

EC-03 — Ownership Control

Memastikan domain ownership tidak berubah secara implisit.

EC-04 — Change Integrity

Memastikan actual change tetap sesuai dengan approved change.

EC-05 — Compliance Control

Memastikan applicable governance requirement tetap dipenuhi.

EC-06 — Traceability Control

Memastikan setiap material evolution dapat ditelusuri.

EC-07 — Compatibility Control

Memastikan compatibility atau migration requirement tetap terkendali.

EC-08 — Deviation Control

Memastikan deviation tidak menjadi unauthorized evolution.

EC-09 — Evidence Control

Memastikan control state memiliki evidence yang memadai.

⸻

6\. Control Boundary

H7 berada di antara decision/review dan implementation:

Decision

↓

Review

↓

CONTROL

↓

Implementation

H7 tidak mengubah decision secara unilateral.

Jika control menemukan material deviation:

Deviation

↓

Control Finding

↓

Assessment

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Correction / Reassessment / Escalation

⸻

7\. In Scope

H7 mencakup:

• approved change scope;

• change baseline;

• control conditions;

• authority conditions;

• ownership conditions;

• compliance conditions;

• implementation gates;

• deviation control;

• change version;

• change status;

• control evidence;

• compatibility control;

• migration control;

• rollback or mitigation requirement;

• change traceability;

• control closure;

• re-control after material deviation.

⸻

8\. Out of Scope

H7 tidak memiliki primary responsibility terhadap:

• deciding whether evolution is strategically required;

• approving evolution;

• independent review conclusion;

• technical implementation;

• operational execution;

• domain ownership;

• final evolution success;

• performance measurement;

• architecture decision authority.

Boundary:

Control

≠

Decision

Control

≠

Review

Control

≠

Implementation

Control

≠

Ownership

⸻

Setiap material evolution yang memasuki H7 harus memiliki control object yang identifiable.

Change ID

10\. Approved Change Baseline

Setiap controlled evolution harus memiliki baseline yang menjelaskan:

• approved change;

• reason;

• owner;

• authority;

• impact;

• conditions;

• version.

Approved Change

↓

Baseline

↓

Control

↓

Implementation

Control tidak boleh bekerja terhadap change definition yang tidak memiliki identifiable baseline.

⸻

11\. Control Before Implementation

Sebelum implementation dimulai, H7 harus memastikan minimum control conditions terpenuhi.

Decision

↓

Review

↓

Control Readiness

│

├── Ready

│ ↓

│ H8 Implementation

│

└── Not Ready

↓

Correction /

Reassessment

H7 tidak memberikan strategic approval.

H7 hanya menentukan apakah control conditions untuk proceed telah terpenuhi.

⸻

12\. Control Conditions

Material evolution minimal harus memiliki:

0\. approved scope;

0\. accountable owner;

0\. applicable authority;

0\. review conclusion;

0\. impact understanding;

0\. compliance conditions;

0\. compatibility position;

0\. implementation boundary;

0\. evidence requirement;

0\. deviation mechanism.

⸻

13\. Control Gates

Evolution Control dapat menggunakan gates:

Gate 1 — Authorization Integrity

↓

Gate 2 — Scope Integrity

↓

Gate 3 — Ownership Integrity

↓

Gate 4 — Impact / Compatibility Integrity

↓

Gate 5 — Implementation Readiness

↓

Gate 6 — Change Validation

↓

Gate 7 — Closure

Gate taxonomy ini merupakan bounded reconstruction dari source-supported controlled-change principles.

⸻

14\. Authorization Integrity Gate

Control harus memverifikasi:

• decision reference tersedia;

• authority legitimate;

• approval applicable;

• approval belum superseded;

• approval scope jelas.

Jika authority tidak jelas:

Authority Unclear

↓

CONTROL BLOCK

↓

Authority Reassessment

Tidak diperbolehkan melanjutkan hanya berdasarkan organizational assumption.

⸻

15\. Scope Integrity Gate

Control harus membandingkan:

Approved Scope

VS

Actual / Proposed Scope

Jika actual change keluar dari approved scope:

Scope Deviation

↓

Control Finding

↓

Stop / Reassess

Scope expansion tidak boleh disahkan secara informal.

⸻

16\. Ownership Integrity Gate

Control harus memeriksa bahwa:

• owner tetap identifiable;

• implementation owner jelas;

• governance owner jelas;

• affected domain owner jelas;

• responsibility tidak berpindah tanpa authority.

Canonical principle:

Governance responsibility does not automatically transfer domain ownership.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

17\. Impact Control

Control harus menjaga agar impact assessment tetap relevan.

Jika material impact berubah:

Changed Impact

↓

Control Finding

↓

Reassessment

↓

Applicable Authority

Control tidak boleh menyembunyikan material impact baru hanya agar implementation dapat dilanjutkan.

⸻

18\. Compatibility Control

Evolution harus mempertahankan compatibility sejauh feasible.

Model:

Existing State

↓

Compatibility Assessment

│

├── Compatible

│ ↓

│ Continue

│

└── Incompatible

↓

Migration /

Mitigation

Baseline mendukung backward compatibility dan migration apabila compatibility tidak dapat dipertahankan.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

19\. Migration Control

Jika migration diperlukan, H7 harus memastikan:

• migration scope;

• migration owner;

• migration dependency;

• migration evidence;

• transition condition;

• validation;

• consequence.

Migration tidak boleh menjadi hidden implementation scope.

⸻

20\. Version Control

Setiap material evolution harus dapat dibedakan berdasarkan version atau equivalent change identity.

Model:

v1

↓

Controlled Change

↓

v2

Perubahan material tidak boleh dilakukan secara silent terhadap baseline yang sama.

PROPOSED

CONTROL_PENDING

CONTROL_READY

IN_IMPLEMENTATION

BLOCKED

DEVIATED

VALIDATION_PENDING

CLOSURE_PENDING

22\. Control Evidence

H7 harus mempertahankan evidence yang menunjukkan:

• control condition;

• change status;

• scope state;

• owner;

• authority;

• deviation;

• mitigation;

• validation.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri kepada evolution record.

⸻

23\. Control Traceability

Canonical chain:

Driver

↓

Change

↓

Decision

↓

Review

↓

Control Baseline

↓

Implementation

↓

Deviation / Evidence

↓

Validation

↓

Closure

Traceability menjaga governance evolution tetap auditable.

Baseline menetapkan traceability sebagai bagian penting dari governance integrity.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

24\. Control Monitoring

H7 harus dapat mengetahui apakah controlled evolution:

• masih within scope;

• masih within authority;

• masih within timeline/condition apabila ditetapkan;

• masih memiliki owner;

• masih memiliki evidence;

• masih compatible;

• mengalami deviation.

Monitoring tidak berarti H7 mengambil operational ownership.

⸻

25\. Control vs Operational Monitoring

Perbedaan:

H7

Governance Change Control

│

▼

Checks governance conditions

Domain Owner

Operational Monitoring

│

▼

Checks domain execution

Keduanya dapat menggunakan evidence yang sama, tetapi memiliki ownership berbeda.

⸻

26\. Deviation

Deviation adalah kondisi ketika actual evolution berbeda secara material dari approved baseline.

Contoh:

• scope berubah;

• owner berubah;

• impact berubah;

• implementation approach materially berubah;

• compatibility assumption berubah;

• authority condition tidak terpenuhi.

⸻

27\. Deviation Classification

Deviation dapat direkonstruksi menjadi:

Minor Deviation

Material Deviation

Critical Deviation

Classification harus ditentukan berdasarkan materiality, impact, dan applicable governance criteria.

Taxonomy ini bukan source literal.

⸻

28\. Minor Deviation

Minor deviation dapat ditangani dalam existing authority apabila:

• tidak mengubah material scope;

• tidak mengubah ownership;

• tidak mengubah authority;

• tidak mengubah material impact;

• tidak menurunkan compliance.

Disposition harus tetap dicatat.

⸻

29\. Material Deviation

Material deviation harus menghasilkan:

Deviation

↓

Control Finding

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Reassessment / Approval

Tidak boleh langsung dianggap sebagai implementation detail.

⸻

30\. Critical Deviation

Critical deviation harus memicu:

• control block;

• escalation;

• authority reassessment;

• mitigation;

• documented consequence.

Implementation tidak boleh dilanjutkan secara normal apabila critical deviation belum memiliki disposition sah.

⸻

31\. Control Block

H7 dapat memberikan control block terhadap continuation ketika control condition material tidak terpenuhi.

Control block berarti:

evolution tidak boleh melanjutkan ke next controlled state sampai blocking condition memiliki disposition.

Control block bukan permanent rejection.

⸻

32\. Reassessment Trigger

Reassessment harus dipertimbangkan apabila:

• authority berubah;

• scope berubah;

• impact berubah;

• ownership berubah;

• compatibility berubah;

• canonical requirement berubah;

• major evidence baru muncul;

• regression muncul;

• implementation materially diverges.

⸻

33\. Reassessment Boundary

Canonical flow:

Control Finding

↓

Reassessment

↓

H5 / H6 / Applicable Authority

↓

New Control Baseline

↓

Continue / Stop

H7 tidak melakukan material decision replacement sendiri.

⸻

34\. Change Freeze

Jika material condition belum jelas:

Unknown Condition

↓

Change Freeze

↓

Evidence / Assessment

↓

Decision

↓

Control Resume

Change freeze digunakan untuk menjaga integrity, bukan sebagai punishment.

⸻

35\. Emergency Change

Jika AIOS memiliki authorized emergency change mechanism, H7 dapat mengontrol emergency evolution dengan accelerated control.

Emergency status tidak menghapus:

• authority;

• evidence;

• traceability;

• post-change review;

• accountability.

Jika baseline tidak menetapkan emergency taxonomy, taxonomy tersebut harus diperlakukan sebagai bounded implementation reconstruction.

⸻

36\. Regression Control

Jika evolution menyebabkan regression:

Regression

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Authority Decision

↓

Mitigation / Migration / Reversal

↓

Validation

Baseline secara eksplisit mensyaratkan rationale, impact assessment, authority decision, migration/mitigation, dan documented consequence ketika regression tidak dapat dihindari.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

37\. Rollback / Reversal

59\. Control Integrity Rules

Rule EC-01

No material evolution without identifiable control baseline.

Rule EC-02

No scope expansion without applicable authority.

Rule EC-03

No silent owner transfer.

Rule EC-04

No material deviation without disposition.

Rule EC-05

No control closure without required evidence.

Rule EC-06

No control mechanism may create domain ownership.

Rule EC-07

No control mechanism may replace Architecture Authority.

Rule EC-08

No control mechanism may bypass canonical authority.

Rule EC-09

No material regression may be hidden.

Rule EC-10

Control evidence must remain traceable.

⸻

60\. Control Anti-Patterns

60.1 Rubber-Stamp Control

Control tidak boleh hanya mencatat status tanpa verification.

60.2 Silent Scope Expansion

Scope tidak boleh berubah tanpa authority.

60.3 Governance Takeover

Control tidak boleh mengambil execution ownership.

60.4 Invisible Deviation

Material deviation harus dicatat.

60.5 Control Without Evidence

Control conclusion harus memiliki evidence.

60.6 Control Without Owner

Material control harus memiliki owner.

60.7 Control Without Closure

Open condition tidak boleh dianggap closed.

60.8 Control as New Authority

H7 tidak menciptakan authority baru.

72\. Canonical Control Contract

H7 menetapkan kontrak:

PD-03 MAY:

\- establish governance evolution controls;

\- define control conditions;

\- monitor approved governance change;

\- detect governance deviation;

\- block continuation where material control conditions fail;

\- require reassessment;

\- preserve control evidence;

\- require validation;

\- close governance control lifecycle.

PD-03 MUST:

\- preserve authority boundaries;

\- preserve ownership;

\- preserve Platform autonomy;

\- maintain traceability;

\- maintain evidence;

\- control material change;

\- document deviation;

\- use applicable authority.

PD-03 MUST NOT:

\- self-authorize material evolution;

\- replace decision authority;

\- replace review authority;

\- perform technical implementation;

\- perform domain execution;

\- silently expand scope;

\- silently transfer ownership;

\- bypass canonical authority.

\### Freeze Decision

\*\*H7 = FROZEN\*\*

Catatan penting untuk menjaga integritas konstruksi: \*\*H7 bukan "control authority" baru\*\*. Ia adalah mekanisme untuk mengendalikan change yang sudah memiliki basis keputusan/review. Ini konsisten dengan baseline yang memisahkan \*\*Governance responsibility\*\* dari \*\*execution ownership\*\* dan menegaskan bahwa control tidak sama dengan execution.

\# 1. Purpose

H7 menetapkan \*\*Evolution Control Architecture\*\* untuk

memastikan bahwa governance evolution yang telah memperoleh

applicable decision dan review dapat dikendalikan secara

terstruktur sebelum dan selama implementation.

H7 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana evolution yang telah diotorisasi dikendalikan

\> agar tetap berada dalam approved scope, authority boundary,

\> ownership boundary, impact boundary, dan governance

\> requirements sampai implementation dan validation selesai?\*\*

H7 bukan:

\- decision authority;

\- review authority;

\- implementation mechanism;

\- operational execution mechanism;

\- final success assessment.

H7 adalah \*\*control layer\*\*.

3\. Definition

Evolution Control adalah mekanisme untuk menjaga agar evolution yang telah disetujui tetap:

• within approved scope;

• within authority;

• within ownership boundary;

• within impact boundary;

• traceable;

• compliant;

• compatible;

• controlled;

• reversible or mitigated where required.

Canonical distinction:

H5

Decision

↓

H6

Review

↓

H7

Control

↓

H8

Implementation

H7 tidak menciptakan approval.

H7 memastikan approval yang telah diberikan tidak berubah menjadi uncontrolled change.

⸻

4\. Source-Supported Foundation

Baseline menetapkan Controlled Change sebagai prinsip evolution.

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa major change minimal memerlukan:

• reason for change;

• impact analysis;

• owner;

• approval;

• versioning.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan:

• authority clarity;

• accountability;

• ownership;

• traceability;

• compliance;

• Platform autonomy;

• canonical consistency.

Jika regression tidak dapat dihindari, diperlukan:

• explicit rationale;

• impact assessment;

• authority decision;

• migration or mitigation;

• documented consequence.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Dengan demikian H7 memiliki foundation source yang kuat meskipun exact H7 heading tidak ditemukan secara literal.

⸻

5\. Control Objective

H7 memiliki objective berikut.

EC-01 — Scope Control

Memastikan implementation tidak keluar dari approved scope.

EC-02 — Authority Control

Memastikan change tetap berada dalam authority yang sah.

EC-03 — Ownership Control

Memastikan domain ownership tidak berubah secara implisit.

EC-04 — Change Integrity

Memastikan actual change tetap sesuai dengan approved change.

EC-05 — Compliance Control

Memastikan applicable governance requirement tetap dipenuhi.

EC-06 — Traceability Control

Memastikan setiap material evolution dapat ditelusuri.

EC-07 — Compatibility Control

Memastikan compatibility atau migration requirement tetap terkendali.

EC-08 — Deviation Control

Memastikan deviation tidak menjadi unauthorized evolution.

EC-09 — Evidence Control

Memastikan control state memiliki evidence yang memadai.

⸻

• decision reference tersedia;

• authority legitimate;

• approval scope jelas.

Authority Unclear

Approved Scope

• owner tetap identifiable;

• implementation owner jelas;

• governance owner jelas;

Setiap material evolution harus dapat dibedakan berdasarkan version atau equivalent change identity.

Model:

v1

↓

Controlled Change

↓

v2

Perubahan material tidak boleh dilakukan secara silent terhadap baseline yang sama.

PROPOSED

CONTROL_PENDING

CONTROL_READY

IN_IMPLEMENTATION

DEVIATED

22\. Control Evidence

H7 harus mempertahankan evidence yang menunjukkan:

• control condition;

• change status;

• scope state;

• owner;

• authority;

• deviation;

• mitigation;

• validation.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri kepada evolution record.

23\. Control Traceability

Canonical chain:

Driver

↓

Change

↓

Decision

↓

Review

↓

Control Baseline

↓

Implementation

↓

↓

Traceability menjaga governance evolution tetap auditable.

Baseline menetapkan traceability sebagai bagian penting dari governance integrity.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

24\. Control Monitoring

H7 harus dapat mengetahui apakah controlled evolution:

• masih within scope;

• masih within authority;

• masih within timeline/condition apabila ditetapkan;

• masih memiliki owner;

• masih memiliki evidence;

• masih compatible;

• mengalami deviation.

Monitoring tidak berarti H7 mengambil operational ownership.

⸻

25\. Control vs Operational Monitoring

Perbedaan:

H7

Governance Change Control

│

▼

Checks governance conditions

Domain Owner

Operational Monitoring

│

▼

Checks domain execution

Keduanya dapat menggunakan evidence yang sama, tetapi memiliki ownership berbeda.

⸻

26\. Deviation

Deviation adalah kondisi ketika actual evolution berbeda secara material dari approved baseline.

Contoh:

• scope berubah;

• owner berubah;

• impact berubah;

• implementation approach materially berubah;

• compatibility assumption berubah;

• authority condition tidak terpenuhi.

⸻

27\. Deviation Classification

Deviation dapat direkonstruksi menjadi:

Minor Deviation

Material Deviation

Critical Deviation

Classification harus ditentukan berdasarkan materiality, impact, dan applicable governance criteria.

Taxonomy ini bukan source literal.

⸻

28\. Minor Deviation

Minor deviation dapat ditangani dalam existing authority apabila:

• tidak mengubah material scope;

• tidak mengubah ownership;

• tidak mengubah authority;

• tidak mengubah material impact;

• tidak menurunkan compliance.

Disposition harus tetap dicatat.

⸻

29\. Material Deviation

Material deviation harus menghasilkan:

Deviation

↓

Control Finding

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Reassessment / Approval

Tidak boleh langsung dianggap sebagai implementation detail.

⸻

30\. Critical Deviation

Critical deviation harus memicu:

• control block;

• escalation;

• authority reassessment;

• mitigation;

• documented consequence.

Implementation tidak boleh dilanjutkan secara normal apabila critical deviation belum memiliki disposition sah.

⸻

31\. Control Block

H7 dapat memberikan control block terhadap continuation ketika control condition material tidak terpenuhi.

Control block berarti:

evolution tidak boleh melanjutkan ke next controlled state sampai blocking condition memiliki disposition.

Control block bukan permanent rejection.

⸻

32\. Reassessment Trigger

Reassessment harus dipertimbangkan apabila:

• authority berubah;

• scope berubah;

• impact berubah;

• ownership berubah;

• compatibility berubah;

• canonical requirement berubah;

• major evidence baru muncul;

• regression muncul;

• implementation materially diverges.

⸻

33\. Reassessment Boundary

Canonical flow:

Control Finding

↓

Reassessment

↓

H5 / H6 / Applicable Authority

↓

New Control Baseline

↓

Continue / Stop

H7 tidak melakukan material decision replacement sendiri.

⸻

34\. Change Freeze

Jika material condition belum jelas:

Unknown Condition

↓

Change Freeze

↓

Evidence / Assessment

↓

Decision

↓

Control Resume

Change freeze digunakan untuk menjaga integrity, bukan sebagai punishment.

⸻

35\. Emergency Change

Jika AIOS memiliki authorized emergency change mechanism, H7 dapat mengontrol emergency evolution dengan accelerated control.

Emergency status tidak menghapus:

• authority;

• evidence;

• traceability;

• post-change review;

• accountability.

Jika baseline tidak menetapkan emergency taxonomy, taxonomy tersebut harus diperlakukan sebagai bounded implementation reconstruction.

Impact Assessment

Tidak setiap evolution wajib memiliki rollback mekanis.

Namun material irreversible change harus memiliki documented consequence dan applicable mitigation.

Role Primary Responsibility

Review

48\. Control Effectiveness

Control effectiveness harus dinilai berdasarkan apakah control mampu:

• mencegah unauthorized change;

• mendeteksi deviation;

• mempertahankan traceability;

• menjaga authority boundary;

• menjaga ownership;

• menjaga compliance;

• menghasilkan evidence.

Control yang selalu bypass tidak dapat dianggap effective.

Finding

50\. Control Evidence Integrity

Evidence control harus:

• identifiable;

• attributable;

• time-aware;

• traceable;

• relevant;

• sufficient.

Evidence yang tidak dapat ditelusuri tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya basis untuk material control conclusion.

51\. Control Closure

H7 control dapat ditutup apabila:

1\. approved change telah selesai atau dispositioned;

2\. material deviation telah resolved/dispositioned;

3\. required validation telah selesai;

4\. evidence telah preserved;

5\. ownership telah jelas;

6\. outstanding action telah assigned;

7\. closure state telah recorded.

59\. Control Integrity Rules

Rule EC-01

No material evolution without identifiable control baseline.

Rule EC-02

No scope expansion without applicable authority.

Rule EC-03

No silent owner transfer.

Rule EC-04

No material deviation without disposition.

Rule EC-05

No control closure without required evidence.

Rule EC-06

No control mechanism may create domain ownership.

Rule EC-07

No control mechanism may replace Architecture Authority.

Rule EC-08

No control mechanism may bypass canonical authority.

Rule EC-09

No material regression may be hidden.

Rule EC-10

Control evidence must remain traceable.

⸻

60\. Control Anti-Patterns

60.1 Rubber-Stamp Control

Control tidak boleh hanya mencatat status tanpa verification.

60.2 Silent Scope Expansion

Scope tidak boleh berubah tanpa authority.

60.3 Governance Takeover

Control tidak boleh mengambil execution ownership.

60.4 Invisible Deviation

Material deviation harus dicatat.

60.5 Control Without Evidence

Control conclusion harus memiliki evidence.

60.6 Control Without Owner

Material control harus memiliki owner.

60.7 Control Without Closure

Open condition tidak boleh dianggap closed.

60.8 Control as New Authority

H7 tidak menciptakan authority baru.

72\. Canonical Control Contract

H7 menetapkan kontrak:

PD-03 MAY:

\- establish governance evolution controls;

\- define control conditions;

\- monitor approved governance change;

\- detect governance deviation;

\- block continuation where material control conditions fail;

\- require reassessment;

\- preserve control evidence;

\- require validation;

\- close governance control lifecycle.

PD-03 MUST:

\- preserve authority boundaries;

\- preserve ownership;

\- preserve Platform autonomy;

\- maintain traceability;

\- maintain evidence;

\- control material change;

\- document deviation;

\- use applicable authority.

PD-03 MUST NOT:

\- self-authorize material evolution;

\- replace decision authority;

\- replace review authority;

\- perform technical implementation;

\- perform domain execution;

\- silently expand scope;

\- silently transfer ownership;

\- bypass canonical authority.

⸻

73\. Control Success Conditions

H7 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. setiap material evolution memiliki identifiable control baseline;

2\. approved scope dapat diverifikasi;

3\. authority dapat diverifikasi;

4\. ownership dapat diverifikasi;

5\. material impact dapat dipantau;

6\. compatibility dapat dipantau;

7\. deviation dapat dideteksi;

8\. deviation memiliki disposition;

9\. control evidence dapat ditelusuri;

10\. validation requirement jelas;

11\. closure condition jelas;

12\. implementation boundary tetap terpisah;

13\. Platform autonomy tetap terjaga;

14\. canonical authority tetap dihormati;

15\. recurring control findings dapat menjadi input improvement.

\### Freeze Decision

\*\*H7 = FROZEN\*\*

Catatan penting untuk menjaga integritas konstruksi: \*\*H7 bukan "control authority" baru\*\*. Ia adalah mekanisme untuk mengendalikan change yang sudah memiliki basis keputusan/review. Ini konsisten dengan baseline yang memisahkan \*\*Governance responsibility\*\* dari \*\*execution ownership\*\* dan menegaskan bahwa control tidak sama dengan execution.

• scope expansion;

• material sequence change;

• owner change;

• dependency change;

• compatibility assumption change;

• material artifact change.

Deviation harus dikembalikan kepada H7.

• tidak mengubah material impact;

• tidak mengurangi compliance.

5\. validation package tersedia;

6\. outstanding condition telah assigned;

7\. handoff ke validation berhasil.

Implementation closure ≠ Evolution Success.

52\. Implementation Governance Rules

Rule EI-01

No implementation without applicable authorization.

Rule EI-02

No implementation outside approved scope.

Rule EI-03

No silent material implementation change.

Rule EI-04

No implementation ownership transfer through governance relationship.

Rule EI-05

No technical ownership creation through governance implementation.

Rule EI-06

No bypass of H7 control for material deviation.

Rule EI-07

No canonical overwrite without versioned traceability.

Rule EI-08

No completion claim without evidence.

Rule EI-09

No validation bypass where validation is required.

Rule EI-10

No implementation may manufacture authority.

⸻

53\. Implementation Anti-Patterns

53.1 Decision by Implementation

Implementation tidak boleh membuat keputusan yang belum disahkan.

53.2 Scope Drift

Implementation tidak boleh memperluas scope secara diam-diam.

53.3 Governance Takeover

PD-03 tidak boleh mengambil execution ownership Platform lain.

53.4 Silent Rewrite

Canonical history tidak boleh dihapus melalui overwrite.

53.5 Evidence-Free Completion

Implementation tidak boleh dinyatakan selesai tanpa evidence yang memadai.

53.6 Validation Bypass

Implementation tidak boleh dianggap successful hanya karena perubahan sudah diterapkan.

53.7 Dependency Concealment

Dependency failure tidak boleh disembunyikan.

53.8 Technical Expansion

Governance implementation tidak boleh berubah menjadi technical architecture implementation.

⸻

Implementation integrity is insufficient.

66\. Canonical Governance Implementation Contract

PD-03 MAY:

\- implement approved governance policy changes;

\- implement approved governance standards changes;

\- implement governance control changes;

\- implement certification mechanism changes;

\- implement compliance mechanism changes;

\- coordinate authorized cross-platform governance implementation;

\- maintain implementation evidence;

\- maintain implementation traceability;

\- coordinate migration or mitigation where authorized;

\- prepare validation.

PD-03 MUST:

\- implement only authorized change;

\- preserve approved scope;

\- preserve ownership boundaries;

\- preserve Platform autonomy;

\- preserve canonical authority;

\- maintain versioning;

\- maintain evidence;

\- route material deviation through control;

\- preserve validation requirements.

PD-03 MUST NOT:

\- create new authority through implementation;

\- self-authorize material evolution;

\- perform technical implementation for another Platform;

\- take operational ownership;

\- replace Architecture Authority;

\- replace domain owner;

\- silently expand scope;

\- bypass control;

\- declare final success.

⸻

67\. Implementation Success Conditions

H8 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. approved evolution telah diimplementasikan;

2\. implementation tetap berada dalam approved scope;

3\. implementation owner jelas;

4\. implementation evidence lengkap;

5\. material deviation tercatat;

6\. deviation telah dispositioned;

7\. compatibility requirement terpenuhi atau dimitigasi;

8\. migration dilakukan jika diperlukan;

9\. canonical artifacts telah diperbarui;

10\. versioning terjaga;

11\. cross-platform ownership tetap terjaga;

12\. Platform autonomy tetap terjaga;

13\. validation package lengkap;

14\. implementation closure dapat dibuktikan;

15\. tidak ada unauthorized authority creation;

16\. implementation dapat ditelusuri kembali ke decision.

⸻

• Governance ≠ Execution.

Bounded Reconstruction

• implementation closure record;

• detailed artifact implementation flow;

Elemen tersebut tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai kutipan literal baseline.
